TL;DR: 提出了首个跨法域法律NLP基准Multi-Legal-Bench，涵盖6个国家、4个语系、1.34亿份判决，评估7个前沿LLM和4个小模型，发现少样本效果跨法域可复制、无单一模型主导所有语言、跨语言迁移不遵循语言 proximity、tokenizer效率不显著预测准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有法律NLP基准大多评估单一语言或聚合不同法域的任务，导致跨语言比较无法进行，亟需一个结构化的跨法域基准来系统评估模型在不同法律体系下的表现。

Method: 构建5×6稀疏任务-法域矩阵（20/30单元格有数据），定义5个任务（法院类型分类、判决形式分类、案件结果预测、法律规范提取、案由分类），映射自6国法院登记册的结构化元数据。通过AWS Bedrock对7个前沿LLM进行零样本和3样本测试，另加4个小模型进行规模分析。

Result: (1) 任务相关的少样本效应在乌克兰发现后在其他法域得到复制验证；(2) 无单一模型在所有语言中占优，排名随任务和法域变化；(3) 跨语言少样本迁移不遵循语言 proximity：UA→FR（罗曼语族，-2.1pp）优于UA→PL（斯拉夫语族，-13.7pp），标签集对齐比语系更能预测迁移质量；(4) tokenizer fertility虽有2.3倍差异，但与跨语言准确率无显著相关（r=-0.27, p=0.14）。

Conclusion: 跨法域法律NLP评估揭示模型性能高度依赖任务和法域组合，模型架构和预训练数据比tokenizer效率对跨语言表现影响更大。数据集、提示和模型预测已全部开源。

Abstract: Legal NLP benchmarks overwhelmingly evaluate a single language or aggregate tasks that differ fundamentally across jurisdictions, making cross-lingual comparison impossible. We introduce Multi-Legal-Bench, the first cross-jurisdictional legal benchmark that evaluates identical tasks across six countries (Ukraine, France, Netherlands, Poland, Czech Republic, Lithuania), four language families, and 134 million court decisions. The benchmark defines five tasks court-type classification, judgment form classification, case-outcome prediction, legal norm extraction, and cause category prediction mapped to structured metadata from national court registries, forming a deliberately sparse 5x6 task-jurisdiction matrix (20 of 30 cells filled). We evaluate 7 frontier LLMs under zero-shot and 3-shot prompting via AWS Bedrock, with 4 additional small/medium models (3-12B) for scaling analysis. Our results reveal that: (1) task-dependent few-shot effects discovered in Ukrainian replicate across all jurisdictions; (2) no single model dominates any language rankings shift with both task and jurisdiction; (3) cross-lingual few-shot transfer does not follow language proximity: UA->FR (Romance, -2.1 pp) transfers better than UA->PL (Slavic, -13.7 pp), with label-set alignment predicting transfer quality better than language family; and (4) tokenizer fertility, despite a 2.3x spread, does not significantly predict cross-lingual accuracy (r=-0.27, p=0.14), suggesting that model architecture and pretraining data dominate tokenizer efficiency. We release all data, prompts, and model predictions.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [76] [Manifold-based Algorithms for the Hadamard Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28980)
*Nicolas Gillis,Subhayan Saha,Stefano Sicilia,Arnaud Vandaele*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究Hadamard分解（HD）问题，即寻找两个低秩矩阵X1和X2，使其元素级乘积近似原矩阵X。作者提出理论见解（如X≈WH^T的重新参数化），并基于此开发三种新算法：基于Manopt的原始形式算法、块投影梯度法和无需投影的流形梯度下降法。后两种特别适合大规模稀疏数据。还提出新的初始化策略提升精度。实验表明新方法在合成和真实数据上高效且有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hadamard分解（HD）相比截断奇异值分解（TSVD）等标准低秩近似更具表达力，因为两个低秩矩阵的Hadamard乘积的秩通常等于r1*r2，能以相同参数数量表示更高秩矩阵。然而现有HD计算方法有限，本文旨在提供理论洞见并开发高效算法，特别是针对大规模稀疏数据场景。

Method: 1) 理论方面：给出X≈X1∘X2的等价重参数化形式X≈WH^T，其中W和H具有r1*r2列且属于特定流形。2) 算法方面：提出三种新算法——基于Manopt工具箱的原始形式优化、基于重参数化的块投影梯度法、以及无需显式投影的流形梯度下降法。3) 提出新的初始化策略以提高HD精度。

Result: 新算法在合成数据和真实数据上均表现高效且具有竞争力。基于重参数化的后两种算法尤其适合处理大规模稀疏数据。新的初始化策略显著提升了HD的精度。

Conclusion: 本文从理论和算法两方面推进了Hadamard分解的研究，提出的三种新算法和初始化策略使得HD在计算上更加实用，尤其是在大规模稀疏数据场景下，为低秩近似提供了比TSVD更强大的替代方案。

Abstract: Given a matrix $X$, and two ranks $r_1$ and $r_2$, the Hadamard decomposition (HD) looks for two low-rank matrices, $X_1$ of rank $r_1$ and $X_2$ of rank $r_2$, both of the same size as $X$, such that $X\approx X_1\circ X_2$, where $\circ$ is the Hadamard (element-wise) product. In most cases, HD is more expressive than standard low-rank approximations such as the truncated singular value decomposition (TSVD), as it can represent higher-rank matrices with the same number of parameters; this is because the rank of $X_1 \circ X_2$ is generically equal to $r_1 r_2$. In this paper, we first present some theoretical insights for HD, in particular a useful reformulation $X\approx WH^\top$ where $W$ and $H$ have $r_1 r_2$ columns and belong to certain manifolds. These allow us to develop three new algorithms for computing HD. The first one uses the representation $X\approx X_1\circ X_2$ and relies on the Manopt toolbox. The other two rely on the reformulation $X\approx WH^\top$: one is a block projected gradient method, and the other is a manifold-based gradient descent algorithm that does not require projection onto the feasible set. The last two algorithms are particularly effective for handling large sparse data. We also propose new initializations that allow us to improve the accuracy of the HD. We compare our algorithms and initialization strategies with the TSVD and with the state of the art. Numerical results show that the new methods are efficient and competitive on both synthetic and real data.

</details>


### [77] [An existence result for a quantitative isoperimetric inequality in $\mathbb{R}^3$ involving the Hausdorff asymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29046)
*Silvio Bove,Gisella Croce,Giovanni Pisante*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了三维空间中涉及豪斯多夫不对称性的定量等周比$\mathcal{Q}_*$的最优存在性问题，证明了在固定体积凸体类$\mathcal{A}$上，$\mathcal{Q}_*$能够达到其最小值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探究三维空间中引入豪斯多夫不对称性作为惩罚项的定量等周比是否存在最优形状，这是对经典等周不等式的精细化研究。

Method: 采用变分法和凸体几何中的紧性论证，在固定体积的凸体类中分析$\mathcal{Q}_*$的下确界可达性。

Result: 证明了在$\mathbb{R}^3$中，对于固定体积的凸体类$\mathcal{A}$，定量等周比$\mathcal{Q}_*$确实存在最小值，即存在凸体达到该下确界。

Conclusion: 该结果确认了三维中该定量等周比的最优存在性，为后续研究最优形状的具体特征奠定了基础。

Abstract: We study the existence of an optimizer for a quantitative isoperimetric ratio $\mathcal{Q}_*$ in $\mathbb{R}^3$ involving the Hausdorff asymmetry. We prove that $\mathcal{Q}_*$ attains its minimum over the class $\mathcal{A}$ of convex bodies of fixed volume.

</details>


### [78] [Restarted Accelerated Primal-Dual Algorithms with Adaptive Stepsizes for Nonlinear Conic Constrained Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29291)
*Necdet Serhat Aybat,Jinxin Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出带重启的加速原始-对偶算法（rAPDB），用于求解凸非线性锥规划（含二次约束二次规划QCQP），该算法仅需一阶信息和矩阵-向量乘积，在KKT映射度量次正则性条件下建立全局线性收敛性，GPU加速下在随机QCQP和核矩阵学习问题上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 凸非线性锥规划（尤其是QCQP）的原始-对偶重构存在非双线性耦合项，现有针对双线性耦合的原始-对偶方法无法适用，亟需能够处理此类问题的高效一阶算法。

Method: 在加速原始-对偶自适应步长搜索算法基础上，设计了定频和自适应两种重启机制，并融合了单调与非单调自适应步长搜索策略。证明了KKT映射度量次正则性下自中心平滑对偶间隙的二次增长性质，并建立了全局线性收敛性。同时给出了度量次正则性在凸多面体锥上非凸问题中成立的充分条件。

Result: 建立了重启算法在度量次正则性下的全局线性收敛理论；证明了度量次正则性在一般非凸问题（凸多面体锥约束）下的充分条件；数值实验表明，非单调自适应步长策略结合GPU加速在随机QCQP和核矩阵学习问题中取得优异性能。

Conclusion: 提出的rAPDB算法有效解决了非线性锥规划中非双线性耦合带来的挑战，理论收敛保证完善，GPU加速版本在实际问题中具有强竞争力。

Abstract: We propose restarted accelerated primal-dual algorithms with (non-monotone) backtracking (rAPDB) for convex nonlinear conic programs, with quadratically constrained quadratic programs (QCQPs) as a special case. Unlike linear and quadratic programs, these problems give rise to convex-concave minimax reformulations with non-bilinear coupling terms; therefore, the existing primal-dual methods for bilinear couplings are not applicable. To address this challenge, we build on the accelerated primal-dual method with adaptive stepsize search -- as it adapts to the local curvature -- and develop both fixed-frequency and adaptive restart schemes, incorporating both monotone and non-monotone adaptive step-size search strategies. The resulting algorithms require only first-order information and matrix-vector products, making them suitable for large-scale and GPU-accelerated implementation. Under metric subregularity of the KKT mapping, we prove a quadratic growth property for a self-centered smoothed duality gap and establish global linear convergence of the proposed restarted methods. We also establish sufficient conditions under which the metric subregularity holds even for general nonconvex problems over convex polyhedral cones. These results are new and may be of independent interest. Numerical experiments on random QCQPs and kernel matrix learning instances show that the proposed methods, especially with non-monotone adaptive stepsizes and GPU acceleration, achieve strong practical performance.

</details>


### [79] [Global optimization of quadratic root-difference minimization under elliptic annulus constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29294)
*Meijia Yang,Yong Xia*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究椭圆环约束下非凸二次根差最小化问题(QR)，通过建立Annulus Brickman定理将其等价转化为二维凸问题(HP)，并提出一种无需恢复步骤的IMGE算法，该算法利用Frank-Wolfe子问题的解直接作为原问题的近似解，实现了高效全局求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非凸二次根差最小化问题在椭圆环约束下传统方法求解困难，计算成本高，特别是大规模问题下缺乏高效的全局求解算法。

Method: 首先建立Annulus Brickman定理将原非凸问题等价转化为二维凸问题(HP)；然后采用Frank-Wolfe算法求解(HP)；关键发现是Frank-Wolfe子问题的解可直接作为原问题的O(1/√k)-近似解，无需求解昂贵的二次系统进行恢复；基于此恢复无关特性，设计了Iterative Minimum Generalized Eigenpair (IMGE)算法。

Result: IMGE算法收敛迅速，在大规模问题上显著优于传统方法。Frank-Wolfe子问题的解被证明是原问题的O(1/√k)-近似解。

Conclusion: 提出的恢复无关特性将算法副产品转化为主要输出，完全绕过了计算昂贵的解恢复步骤，为椭圆环约束下的非凸二次根差最小化问题提供了高效的全局求解方案。

Abstract: This paper studies the nonconvex quadratic root-difference minimization under elliptic annulus constraints {\rm (QR)}. We first establish the Annulus Brickman theorem and equivalently reformulate {\rm (QR)} as a 2-dimensional convex problem {\rm (HP)} with hidden variables. We employ the Frank-Wolfe algorithm to globally solve {\rm (HP)}. A key finding is that the solutions of the Frank-Wolfe subproblems, which are traditionally viewed as mere auxiliary updates, are proven to be $O(1/\sqrt{k})$-approximate solutions of the original problem {\rm (QR)}. This transforms an algorithmic by-product into the primary output and completely bypasses the need to solve the computationally expensive quadratic system required for solution recovery. Leveraging this recovery-free property, we develop the efficient Iterative Minimum Generalized Eigenpair (IMGE) algorithm for globally solving {\rm (QR)}. Numerical experiments confirm that IMGE converges rapidly and significantly outperforms conventional methods, especially for large-scale problems.

</details>


### [80] [MoSSP: A Momentum-Based Single-Loop Stochastic Penalty Method for Nonconvex Constrained DC-Regularized Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29635)
*Luxuan Li,Chunfeng Cui,Xiao Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了MoSSP算法，一种基于动量的单循环随机罚方法，用于求解带有DC正则化的非凸约束随机优化问题，实现了O(ε^{-4})和O(ε^{-3})的oracle复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单循环算法高效求解无约束DC优化问题，但在带非凸约束的DC结构化优化问题中，保持可行性同时获得良好oracle复杂度仍是一个挑战，该领域尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出MoSSP算法，核心思想是对罚函数的Moreau包络加上凸DC部分执行单步随机近端梯度更新，同时并行计算凹DC部分的近端映射。给出了两种变体：Polyak动量版本（O(ε^{-4})复杂度）和递归动量版本（O(ε^{-3})复杂度）。

Result: Polyak动量版本达到O(ε^{-4})的oracle复杂度，递归动量版本达到O(ε^{-3})的oracle复杂度，用于寻找随机ε-KKT点。实验验证了算法的有效性。

Conclusion: MoSSP成功将单循环动量方法推广到带DC正则化的非凸约束随机优化问题，提供了理论保证和实验验证，填补了该领域的空白。

Abstract: In this paper, we study a structured class of nonconvex constrained stochastic problems with difference-of-convex (DC) regularization, where the feasible set is possibly nonconvex and the concave part of the DC regularizer is allowed to be nonsmooth. The fundamental challenge lies in maintaining feasibility for nonconvex constraints while achieving favorable oracle complexity. Although single-loop algorithms efficiently solve unconstrained DC optimization problems, their potential for constrained optimization with DC structure remains largely unexplored. To address this gap, we develop MoSSP, a Momentum-based Single-loop Stochastic Penalty method for such problems with provable complexity guarantees. The key idea is to apply a single stochastic proximal-gradient step to the Moreau envelope of the penalty plus the convex DC part, with the concave part's proximal mapping computed in parallel. We derive two algorithm variants: a Polyak-momentum version with $O(\varepsilon^{-4})$ oracle complexity for finding stochastic $\varepsilon$-KKT points, and an improved $O(\varepsilon^{-3})$ version incorporating recursive momentum. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed algorithms.

</details>


### [81] [Solving mathematical programs with complementarity constraints by disjunctive regularizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29757)
*Sebastian Lämmel,Vladimir Shikhman*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于带互补约束的数学规划问题（MPCC）的析取正则化方法，该方法在可行集上与Kanzow-Schwartz正则化一致，但函数描述不同。析取正则化满足原始MPCC的线性独立约束规范，且具有良好的收敛性质，无需二阶必要条件即可收敛到S-稳定点。此外，该方法在C-指标方面保持了逼近点和极限点非退化C-稳定点的拓扑类型对应，特别是二次部分和双活动部分在正则化下具有一般性对应关系。数值实验表明，析取正则化显著优于Kanzow-Schwartz正则化，在高精度求解MPCC时甚至优于Scholtes正则化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MPCC正则化方法（如Kanzow-Schwartz、Scholtes）存在局限性：Kanzow-Schwartz正则化不满足线性独立约束规范（LICQ），而Scholtes正则化在高精度求解时性能不足。本文旨在设计一种既能保留原始MPCC的LICQ性质，又具有优越收敛性能和数值效果的正则化方法。

Method: 提出析取正则化方法，利用逻辑OR算子和等价的最大值型约束来描述可行集，这与Kanzow-Schwartz正则化的函数描述有本质区别。在理论分析上，证明了该方法在原始MPCC满足LICQ时也满足定制的LICQ，并研究了C-指标的拓扑保持性质。

Result: 析取正则化保持了Kanzow-Schwartz正则化的收敛性质（无需二阶必要条件即可收敛到S-稳定点），同时额外满足LICQ。C-指标的二次部分和双活动部分在正则化下具有一般性对应关系，这是与Scholtes或符号型正则化不同的新发现。数值实验显示该方法全面优于Kanzow-Schwartz，高精度下甚至优于Scholtes。

Conclusion: 析取正则化是一种理论上更优（满足LICQ）、收敛性质良好且数值表现突出的MPCC正则化方法，尤其在高精度求解场景下具有明显优势。

Abstract: We propose a new disjunctive regularization for mathematical programs with complementarity constraints (MPCC). Its feasible set coincides with that of the Kanzow-Schwartz regularization. However, their functional descriptions differ considerably. For the disjunctive regularization, the logical operator OR and equivalent max-type constraints are used. Unlike the Kanzow-Schwartz, the disjunctive regularization satisfies the tailored linear independence constraint qualification if the original MPCC does. More than that, the favorable convergence properties - known to hold for the Kanzow-Schwartz regularization - remain valid for the disjunctive regularization as well. In particular, no second order necessary conditions are required to guarantee convergence towards S-stationary points of MPCC. Additionally, we keep track of the topological type of approximating and limiting nondegenerate C-stationary points in terms of their C-indices. Quadratic and biactive parts of the C-indices are shown to generically correspond to each other while regularizing. This is a new phenomenon as compared to the Scholtes or sign-type regularizations studied before. Numerical experiments illustrate that the proposed disjunctive regularization clearly outperforms the Kanzow-Schwartz regularization. Its numerical performance is even better than that of the Scholtes regularization if solving MPCCs with high accuracy.

</details>


### [82] [A New Constraint Qualification for Continuous-Time Nonlinear Programming Based on Asymptotic KKT Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29898)
*Rodrigo B. Moreira,Moisés R. C. do Monte,Valeriano A. de Oliveira*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的约束规格——AKKT-正则性，它能使满足AKKT最优性条件的解达到经典KKT条件的精度，且是该类性质中最弱的约束规格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AKKT条件虽然无需约束规格即可成立，但生成的候选解不够鲁棒，甚至可能不是稳定点。因此需要一种约束规格来精炼候选解集，使其达到经典KKT条件的精度。

Method: 引入一种新的约束规格——AKKT-正则性，通过理论推导证明在该约束规格下每个局部最优解都满足KKT条件，并验证其是该类性质中最弱的约束规格，同时给出其适用性的充分条件。

Result: 在AKKT-正则性下，每个局部最优解均满足KKT条件；该约束规格是保证该性质的最弱约束规格；提供了其适用性的充分条件。

Conclusion: AKKT-正则性能够有效精炼AKKT条件的候选解集，使其达到经典KKT条件的精度，且是最弱的这种约束规格，具有理论价值与应用潜力。

Abstract: The asymptotic Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (AKKT) optimality conditions are distinguished from other approaches in the literature by virtue of their capacity to be effectively derived through numerical methods, such as the utilization of an appropriate version of the augmented Lagrange method. These tools are of a theoretical nature, yet they possess practical utility in the identification of candidate solutions to continuous-time programming problems. While this type of optimality condition is valid without imposing any constraint qualification, it is not sufficiently robust to generate good candidate solutions. In some cases, solutions satisfying AKKT conditions are not even stationary. In this study, we investigate conditions that effectively refine this set of candidate solutions with the same precision as the classical Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) conditions. This is achieved by introducing a novel constraint qualification, designated AKKT-regularity. It has been demonstrated that, under AKKT-regularity, each local optimal solution is shown to satisfy the KKT conditions. In addition, it is demonstrated that this constraint qualification is the weakest possible to ensure such a property. Furthermore, sufficient conditions are provided for its applicability.

</details>


### [83] [Accelerating Sinkhorn for Entropy-Regularized Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30267)
*Zeyi Xu,Long Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出Acc-Sinkhorn，一种熵正则化最优传输的加速Sinkhorn变体，通过双层优化视角推导出保持Sinkhorn缩放形式的Nesterov加速方法，在可验证稳定性条件下达到O(1/k²)收敛率，将未正则化OT的ε-近似复杂度从Õ(n²/ε²)降至Õ(n²/ε)，实验实现10-30倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Sinkhorn算法收敛速度慢，特别在正则化系数较小时需要大量迭代才能达到高精度；现有加速方法往往破坏了Sinkhorn简单高效的缩放形式。作者希望通过保持Sinkhorn的缩放结构和单步成本的同时，利用Nesterov加速技术提升收敛速度。

Method: 从双层优化视角出发：将Sinkhorn的行缩放视为精确求解内变量u，定义约化对偶目标f(v)=min_u F(u,v)；列缩放视为v上的单位步长对偶镜像下降。基于此Hessian驱动结构，对列缩放步骤进行Nesterov加速，保持Sinkhorn的缩放形式和每次迭代的计算成本，仅使用外推组合的Sinkhorn迭代。

Result: 理论上证明了在可验证稳定性条件下O(1/k²)收敛率；未正则化OT的ε-近似复杂度为Õ(n²/ε)，优于Sinkhorn的Õ(n²/ε²)。在合成问题、颜色迁移和词对齐任务上，小正则化参数下相比标准Sinkhorn获得10-30倍加速。

Conclusion: Acc-Sinkhorn有效克服了Sinkhorn在小正则化下收敛慢的瓶颈，为实际应用中的大规模最优传输问题提供了高效的实用算法。

Abstract: We propose Acc-Sinkhorn, a simple accelerated variant of Sinkhorn for entropy-regularized optimal transport (EOT). The method is derived from a bilevel optimization view: Sinkhorn row scaling solves the inner variable $u$ exactly and defines the reduced dual objective $f(v)=\min_u F(u,v)$, while the remaining column scaling is a unit-step dual mirror descent step in $v$. This structure yields a Hessian-driven Nesterov acceleration that keeps Sinkhorn's scaling form and per-iteration cost, using only extrapolated combinations of Sinkhorn iterates. We prove an $\mathcal{O}(1/k^2)$ rate under a verifiable stability condition. For an $\varepsilon$-approximation of unregularized OT, the resulting complexity is $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(n^2/\varepsilon)$, improved from $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(n^2/\varepsilon^2)$ for Sinkhorn. On synthetic problems, color transfer, and word alignment, Acc-Sinkhorn gives a $10\times$--$30\times$ speedup over Sinkhorn at small regularization.

</details>


### [84] [Proper efficiency results in vector optimisation in real linear-topological spaces based on vectorial penalisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30286)
*Paul Schmölling,Christian Günther,Christiane Tammer,Elisabeth Köbis*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究实线性拓扑空间中带约束向量优化问题的恰当有效解，通过向量惩罚方法，在目标函数满足锥凸性假设下，探讨约束与无约束问题恰当有效解集之间的关系。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究带约束向量优化问题中恰当有效解集与无约束问题中恰当有效解集之间的关系，以在锥凸性假设下建立两者间的理论联系。

Method: 采用向量惩罚方法，在目标函数满足锥凸性假设的条件下，分析约束与无约束向量优化问题之间的解集关系。

Result: 在锥凸性假设下，通过向量惩罚方法建立了约束与无约束向量优化问题恰当有效解集之间的关联。

Conclusion: 向量惩罚方法可有效用于在锥凸性条件下连接约束与无约束向量优化问题的恰当有效解集。

Abstract: In this paper, we are dealing with constrained vector optimisation problems where the objective function acts between real linear-topological spaces. Our aim is to study the relationships between the sets of properly efficient solutions to constrained and unconstrained vector optimisation problems under certain cone convexity assumptions on the objective function using a vectorial penalisation approach.

</details>

### [85] [One Mask to Rule Them All: On Hidden Facts after Editing and How to Find Them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28839)
*Ali Holmov,Paul Youssef,Nandi Schoots,Christin Seifert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了ROME和MEMIT等知识编辑方法在修改Transformer MLP权重时的内部机制，发现无论修改何种事实，这些方法都作用于一个共同的权重子集。通过训练二元掩码可逆转80%训练集和70%测试集的编辑，表明编辑通过抑制而非覆盖知识实现，这解释了为何编辑无法传播到相关事实。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识编辑方法（如ROME和MEMIT）主要通过输出行为评估，其内部机制尚未被充分探索。本文旨在揭示不同事实编辑是否共享共同机制，并理解编辑如何改变模型内部表征。

Method: 训练一个紧凑的二元掩码作用于编辑后的权重上，通过逆转编辑来识别共享的功能结构。分析掩码对注意力模式的影响，并通过注入掩码验证该机制对编辑成功的必要性。

Result: 掩码可逆转80%训练集和70%测试集的编辑；注入掩码导致编辑成功率从98%骤降至38%；掩码通过消除后期层的过度注意力来逆转编辑。

Conclusion: 编辑通过抑制而非覆盖知识实现，这一发现解释了ROME和MEMIT无法将更改传播到相关事实的原因，并识别出的公共功能子空间可用于检测和防御不必要的编辑。

Abstract: Knowledge editing methods such as ROME and MEMIT update factual associations in transformer models by modifying MLP weights. While evaluated mainly by output behavior, their internal mechanism remains underexplored. We investigate whether edits rely on a common mechanism, regardless of which fact is modified. Despite fact-specific weight changes, we argue that ROME and MEMIT target the same subset of weights critical for maintaining edits. To isolate this subset, we train a compact binary mask over the edited weights. The mask reverses 80% of edits on the training set and over 70% on the test set, confirming that diverse edits share a common functional structure. Our analysis reveals that the mask reverses edits by eliminating overattention in later layers. Additionally, we show that injecting the mask during editing drops editing success from 98% to 38%, demonstrating that this mechanism is necessary for edits to succeed. Our finding that edits suppress rather than overwrite knowledge explains why ROME and MEMIT fail to propagate changes to related facts. The identified common functional subspace informs detection and defense against unwanted edits.

</details>


### [86] [Representation Signatures and Risk-Feedback Alignment in LLM Trading Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28850)
*Weicheng Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过TradeArena测试平台研究LLM智能体在金融决策中的行为对齐与表征动态，发现失败前可测量的表征漂移、有效秩收缩等预警信号，并验证了结构化风险反馈作为外部对齐信号的作用，同时揭示了LLM在相关资产风险暴露上的认知盲区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM智能体在金融决策环境中的行为对齐程度和表征动态，尤其是在市场压力下理性、持仓和干预如何演变，以识别潜在的失败前兆。

Method: 使用TradeArena可审计交易智能体测试平台（含风险报告、执行模拟、记忆和可回放轨迹），通过80个滚动失败锚点对8条LLM轨迹进行分析，采用哈希、LSA、Transformer和白盒隐藏状态探针进行嵌入分析，并进行压力测试（去CoT、词汇控制、OHLCV噪声、虚假审计报告）。

Result: 发现可测量的失败前信号：规划嵌入偏离正常状态质心、融合计划-风险表征区分正常与预回撤状态、有效秩收缩。结构化风险反馈可改善部分模型的校准度、收益和回撤，但非通用性能增强器。51只股票日内实验揭示LLM理由常为被风险层反复截断的集中持仓提供正当化解释。

Conclusion: 可审计的风险反馈和表征轨迹能够揭示LLM金融推理何时在对齐、漂移或失败，支撑的是研究主张而非盈利主张。

Abstract: We study behavioral alignment and representation dynamics of large language model (LLM) agents in financial decision environments. Using TradeArena, an auditable trading-agent testbed with risk reports, execution simulation, memory, and replayable trajectories, we analyze how rationales, positions, and interventions evolve under market stress. We find measurable pre-failure signatures: planning embeddings drift from normal-state centroids, fused plan-risk representations separate normal from pre-drawdown states, and manifold diagnostics show effective-rank contraction before failures. To address small-sample and embedding-choice concerns, we use 80 rolling failure anchors across eight LLM trajectories and show that contraction persists across hash, LSA, Transformer, and white-box hidden-state probes. Stress tests with CoT-free target weights, lexical controls, OHLCV noise, and false-audit reports indicate that rationale-level contraction can vanish without rationales, while intent-space contraction may remain; lexical diversity does not collapse; and fused signatures remain informative under noise. We also find that structured risk feedback can act as an external alignment signal without fine-tuning, but not as a universal performance enhancer: true audit feedback improves calibration for some models, return and drawdown for others, and reveals cases where hidden or placebo feedback has higher short-horizon return but weaker alignment diagnostics. Finally, a 51-stock intraday experiment reveals a correlation blind spot: LLM rationales often justify concentrated exposure to coupled assets that the risk layer repeatedly clips, with a rolling Markowitz baseline as a covariance reference. These results support a research claim rather than a profitability claim: auditable risk feedback and representation trajectories reveal when LLM financial reasoning is aligning, drifting, or failing.

</details>


### [87] [Molecular Lead Optimization via Agentic Tool Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28862)
*Lingxiao Li,Haobo Zhang,Ruohao Fan,Bin Chen,Jiayu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE，一种轨迹感知的LLM推理智能体，将分子先导优化的工具选择建模为序列决策问题，在保持分子相似性的同时显著提升ADMET性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分子先导优化方法多为单步优化，无法考虑序列设计决策的长期后果，亟需能够进行前瞻性规划的智能优化方法。

Method: 提出TRACE框架，利用大语言模型作为推理智能体，将分子优化工具的选择建模为动作轨迹上的序列决策问题，实现结构约束下的前瞻性分子优化。

Result: 在多个ADMET优化任务上，TRACE相比基线模型取得了更高的优化成功率、更大的性质提升和更高的有效性，同时保持了分子相似性。

Conclusion: 轨迹感知的序列决策方法能更有效地解决先导化合物的多属性优化问题，为AI驱动的药物发现提供了新范式。

Abstract: Drug discovery is a lengthy and resource-intensive process composed of multiple stages. Among these stages, lead optimization plays a critical role in transforming early hit compounds into viable drug candidates. This stage requires improving ADMET-related properties through subtle structural refinement while preserving key molecular substructures responsible for binding affinity to disease targets. Recent advances in artificial intelligence have shown promise in accelerating various aspects of drug discovery; however, most existing approaches to lead optimization rely on one-step molecular optimization, which fail to account for the long-term consequences of sequential design decisions. To address this limitation, we propose TRACE, a trajectory-aware, LLM-reasoning agent for molecular lead optimization that formulates tool selection as a sequential decision-making problem over action trajectories. Given a lead molecule and an optimization objective, TRACE makes trajectory-aware decisions over molecular optimization tools, enabling forward-looking refinement under structural constraints. Experiments on multiple ADMET optimization tasks show that our agent achieves higher optimization success, larger property improvements, and higher validity, while preserving molecular similarity compared to baseline models.

</details>


### [88] [Self-Play Reinforcement Learning under Imperfect Information in Big 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28863)
*Aalok Patwa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在四人非完全信息卡牌游戏Big 2中，通过自博弈强化学习框架比较多种算法，发现PPO在多种对手类型下优于MC Q近似、SARSA和Q-learning，并验证了适度熵正则化和当前策略自博弈的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非完全信息多人博弈中智能体需要在隐藏信息、稀疏奖励和非平稳对手条件下做出决策，现有研究缺乏对此类复杂游戏环境的系统可控比较。

Method: 在Big 2游戏中构建统一的自我博弈强化学习框架，包括相同的环境、输入表示、训练预算和评估协议，对比策略梯度（PPO）与值近似方法（MC Q近似、SARSA、Q-learning）的性能。

Result: PPO在对抗随机、贪婪和启发式对手时均优于MC Q近似、SARSA和Q-learning；适度熵正则化能防止策略过于确定性而提升PPO性能；当前策略自博弈比检查点自博弈和固定对手训练提供更强的有限预算课程。

Conclusion: Big 2是一个有用的受控实验环境，可用于研究非完全信息、多人交互、延迟奖励和可变动作集下的深度强化学习。

Abstract: Imperfect-information multiplayer games test whether agents can act under hidden information, sparse rewards, and non-stationary opponents. We study these challenges in Big 2, a four-player imperfect-information card game. We develop a self-play RL framework for Big 2 that enables controlled comparisons between policy-gradient and value-approximating agents. Under a common environment, input representation, training budget, and evaluation protocol, PPO outperforms Monte Carlo Q approximation, SARSA, and Q-learning against random, greedy, and heuristic Big 2 opponents. We further find that moderate entropy regularization improves PPO by preventing the policy from becoming overly deterministic, and that current-policy self-play provides a stronger finite-budget curriculum than checkpoint self-play or fixed-opponent training. Together, these results show that Big 2 is a useful controlled setting for studying deep RL under imperfect information, multiplayer interaction, delayed rewards, and variable action sets.

</details>


### [89] [Emergent Semantic Representations in World Models through Physical Interaction without Linguistic Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28865)
*Jiayi Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现，基于VAE的世界模型在无语言监督的随机物理探索中，其潜在空间会自动形成与物理几何结构对齐的空间语义结构，且预测性能与语义对齐共同演化，验证了物理几何是世界模型表征的组织原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究世界模型在纯物理探索（无语言监督）下学到了什么，以及其潜在空间的组织原则。

Method: 训练基于VAE的世界模型进行随机具身探索，通过方向精度、位置RSA等指标评估潜在空间的几何结构；设置20个时间检查点分析预测性能与语义对齐的共变关系；进行KL正则化双敲除实验（beta=0.1 vs 0.001）验证共享驱动假说。

Result: 训练后的编码器方向精度0.677（随机基线0.547），位置RSA 0.192（随机基线0.029，提升6.6倍）；预测性能与语义对齐显著共变（Spearman r=-0.61, p=0.004）；标准KL正则化（beta=0.1）导致几何结构丧失，预测与语义同时崩溃；降低beta至0.001可恢复两者。

Conclusion: 物理世界几何结构是世界模型表征的核心组织原则，对设计具有语义基础的具身智能体有直接指导意义。

Abstract: What does a world model learn from physical exploration, without any linguistic supervision? We argue the answer is organized by a single principle: the geometric structure of the physical world. Training a VAE-based world model on random embodied exploration, we find that its latent space develops spatial semantic structure that mirrors physical geometry -- direction accuracy 0.677+-0.029 versus 0.547 for a randomly initialized encoder, and position RSA 0.192+-0.047 versus 0.029 for random encoders (6.6x improvement), showing that training induces genuine structural organization beyond CNN inductive bias. Across 20 temporal checkpoints, prediction performance and semantic alignment co-improve (Spearman r=-0.61, p=0.004), consistent with the shared-driver account. We confirm this through a double knockout: standard KL regularization (beta=0.1) forces the encoder away from geometric structure, and both prediction performance and semantic alignment collapse simultaneously to near-chance by step 50,000 -- exactly as the shared-driver account predicts. Reducing beta to 0.001 restores geometric access and recovers both capabilities together. These findings establish physical world geometry as the organizing principle of world model representations, with direct implications for the design of semantically grounded embodied agents.

</details>


### [90] [Continuity and Ordinality Matter: Constraining Time Series Tokens for Effective Time Series Analysis with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28866)
*Musheng Li,Ziying Zhang,Cheng jin,Yuantao Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出COM策略，通过几何约束在初始化和训练阶段保留时间序列token的连续性和有序性，显著提升了基于token的时间序列大语言模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于token的时间序列大语言模型忽略了时间序列token固有的连续性和有序性，限制了模型性能。本文认为保留这些属性对于token-based TS-LLMs的有效性至关重要。

Method: 提出COM（Continuity and Ordinality Matter）策略，将几何约束整合到token嵌入的初始化和训练两个阶段中，以保持时间序列token的连续性和有序性。

Result: 在多个时间序列分析基准上的实验结果表明，COM能够持续提升token-based TS-LLMs的性能，取得了有竞争力的结果和强大的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 保留时间序列token的连续性和有序性对于token-based TS-LLMs的有效性至关重要，COM策略通过几何约束有效实现了这一目标。

Abstract: Token-based time series large language models (TS-LLMs) have emerged as a promising direction for time series analysis and reasoning. However, prior studies largely overlook the inherent continuity and ordinality of time series tokens, which substantially limits model performance. In this paper, we argue that preserving these properties in time series token embeddings is crucial for the effectiveness of token-based TS-LLMs. To this end, we propose COM (Continuity and Ordinality Matter), a continuity- and ordinality-aware strategy that integrates geometric constraints into both the initialization and training stages. Empirical results on multiple time series analysis benchmarks demonstrate that COM consistently improves the performance of token-based TS-LLMs, achieving competitive results and strong generalizability. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/COM .

</details>


### [91] [PrismFlow: Residual Dynamics for Flow Matching in Time-Series Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28867)
*Junru Zhang,Lang Feng,Jinbo Wang,Xu Guo,Yucheng Wang,Han Yu,Min Wu,Yabo Dong,Duanqing Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对流匹配（Flow Matching）在异质多模态时间序列数据中因单一全局向量场估计器导致的谱收缩和模式覆盖不足问题，本文提出PrismFlow方法，通过Koopman启发的动力学专家学习潜在空间中的残差修正，并采用置信度感知的胜者全取（WTA）目标实现模态特异性专业化，在多个基准上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界时间序列信号通常呈现多模态模式和多尺度动力学（包括振荡和高频变化），而标准流匹配方法依赖单一有限容量的全局向量场估计器。在异质时序分布中，不同模态可能经过相近的流状态但需要不兼容的条件速度，$\ell_2$速度匹配目标导致估计器学习过于平滑的局部传输场近似，从而削弱分支特定动态，引发谱失真和模式覆盖不足。

Method: 提出PrismFlow方法，包含三个核心组件：(1) Koopman启发的动力学专家，在潜在空间中学习残差修正，使局部非线性时间演化可近似为线性转换；(2) 置信度感知的胜者全取（WTA）目标，仅更新与每个样本最匹配的专家，对其余专家进行梯度掩码，鼓励模态特异性专业化；(3) 采样时，所选专家向全局传输场添加残差动力学修正，在保持FM稳定性的同时恢复细粒度高频时序结构。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，PrismFlow有效缓解了标准流匹配中的谱收缩问题，取得SOTA性能：Context-FID提升15.6%，判别分数（Discriminative Score）改进38.6%，同时在低数据场景下保持鲁棒性，并有效支持时序预测和插补任务。

Conclusion: PrismFlow通过动力学专家残差修正和置信度感知WTA训练，在不破坏FM稳定性的前提下恢复了被平滑掉的分支特定动态和高频结构，为多模态多尺度时间序列生成提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Generating high-quality time-series data is challenging because real-world signals often exhibit multimodal patterns and multiscale dynamics, including oscillations and high-frequency variations. Flow Matching (FM) offers an efficient alternative to diffusion models, but practical implementations typically rely on a single finite-capacity global vector-field estimator. In such heterogeneous temporal distributions, distinct regimes may pass through nearby flow states while requiring incompatible conditional velocities. A monolithic estimator trained with the standard $\ell_2$ velocity-matching objective may therefore learn an overly smoothed approximation of the local transport field. This estimator-level smoothing can attenuate branch-specific dynamics, leading to spectral distortion and poor mode coverage. To address this, we propose PrismFlow, a new FM method with Koopman-inspired dynamical experts. Each expert learns residual corrections in a latent space where local nonlinear temporal evolution can be approximated by linear transitions. We further propose a confidence-aware Winner-Take-All (WTA) objective that updates only the expert best aligned with each sample while masking gradients to the others, encouraging mode-specific specialization. During sampling, the selected expert adds a residual dynamical correction to the global transport field, preserving FM stability while recovering fine-grained and high-frequency temporal structures. Across various benchmarks, PrismFlow effectively mitigates the spectral contraction in standard FM and achieves state-of-the-art performance, with a 15.6% gain in Context-FID and a 38.6% improvement in Discriminative Score, while remaining robust in low-data settings and effective for forecasting and imputation.

</details>


### [92] [Balancing Multimodal Learning through Label Space Reshaping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28869)
*Xiaoyu Ma,Weijie Zhang,Yuanhao Gao,Han Miao,Yongjian Deng,Hao Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多模态学习中的模态不平衡问题，本文提出首个从标签侧设计的平衡多模态标签重塑方法（BMLR），通过重塑跨模态标签空间来均衡各模态的映射难度，促进模态交互并注入更丰富的类间信息，实验证明该方法能持续提升多模态性能并具有良好的模型兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要通过强化弱模态或调整优化梯度来缓解模态不平衡，但未能分析模态层面差异的产生根源，且往往牺牲强模态的优化能力。本文旨在从根源上解释学习速度差异源于各模态特征空间到共享标签空间映射难度的不同，并据此设计解决方案。

Method: 提出Balanced Multimodal Label Reshaping (BMLR)方法，从标签侧出发，通过重塑跨模态标签空间来均衡不同模态的映射难度，从而促进模态交互并为每个模态注入更丰富的类间信息。

Result: 在多种架构上进行的大量实验表明，BMLR能够持续提升多模态学习性能，并展现出与多种模型设计良好的兼容性。

Conclusion: 从标签侧设计的多模态平衡方法（BMLR）有效解决了模态不平衡问题，为多模态学习提供了一种全新视角，且不牺牲强模态的优化能力。

Abstract: Multimodal learning often suffers from modality imbalance, where modalities that converge faster dominate optimization while others remain undertrained. Existing approaches typically mitigate this issue by strengthening the weak modality or adjusting optimization gradients. However, such strategies mainly compensate for optimization rate discrepancies, often at the expense of the strong modality's optimization capacity, without analyzing how these discrepancies arise at the modality level. Based on theoretical insights and empirical observations, we argue that the discrepancy of learning pace arises from differences in the mapping difficulty between modality-specific feature space and the shared label space. To address this issue, we propose Balanced Multimodal Label Reshaping (BMLR), the first method that promotes multimodal balance from the label-side design. BMLR reshapes the cross-modal label space to equalize mapping difficulty across modalities, thereby facilitating modality interaction and injecting richer inter-class information into each modality. Extensive experiments across multiple architectures demonstrate that BMLR consistently improves multimodal performance and exhibits strong compatibility with diverse model designs. The source code will be released soon.

</details>


### [93] [Representation Alignment Rests on Linear Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28870)
*Kiril Bangachev,Guy Bresler,Yury Polyanskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过信号、偏置和噪声的三部分统计框架研究柏拉图表征假说(PRH)，提出普拉顿对齐源于对象-属性的线性表征(LRH)，通过稀疏自编码器验证了LRH对PRH的解释力；发现中心化和归一化可部分缓解模型偏置差异；表征噪声主要由数据稀缺驱动；最后提出了一个细化LRH的统计模型来解释现代AI架构中的表征对齐现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究柏拉图表征假说(PRH)的形成机制，即为什么不同AI模型会趋向于学习相似的表征空间。

Method: 构建信号、偏置、噪声三部分统计框架：(1) 信号部分：基于线性表征假说(LRH)，使用稀疏自编码器提取线性对象-属性特征，比较稀疏与稠密表征的跨模态对齐程度；(2) 偏置部分：研究中心化和归一化对不同架构模型表征对齐的影响；(3) 噪声部分：分析词频与LLM及文本嵌入模型中对齐程度的相关性。

Result: (1) 稀疏表征比稠密表征展现出更强的跨模态对齐；(2) 中心化和归一化能一致地改善跨模型对齐；(3) 词频与对齐程度之间存在强且一致的正相关关系，表明表征噪声由数据稀缺驱动。

Conclusion: 提出了一个细化线性表征假说(PRH)的统计模型，能够解释现代多样AI架构中涌现的表征对齐现象。

Abstract: We investigate the Platonic Representation Hypothesis (PRH) through a tripartite statistical framework of representations: signal, bias, and noise. {1) Signal:} We propose that Platonic alignment arises from the universal relationship between objects and attributes, which is encoded linearly in representations according to the Linear Representation Hypothesis (LRH). We provide evidence that LRH helps explain PRH by extracting linear object-attribute features with sparse autoencoders and showing that these sparse representations often exhibit stronger cross-modal alignment than their dense counterparts. {2) Bias:} Models have different implicit biases due to the diverse architectures and training procedures used. We show that this difference can be partially mitigated. Centering and normalization consistently improve cross-model alignment. {3) Noise:} Finite-sample training leads to noise in representations. We provide evidence that representational noise is driven by data scarcity by revealing a strong and consistent positive correlation between word frequency and alignment in LLMs and text embedding models. Synthesizing signal, bias, and noise, we propose a statistical model that refines the Linear Representation Hypothesis and explains further phenomena related to the alignment of representations emerging from diverse modern AI architectures.

</details>


### [94] [Pre-Registering the Detectable Effect: A Paired-MDE Budget for 4-bit Quantization Benchmarks, with a Pilot Audit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28873)
*Zexin Zhuang,Yanhang Li,Zhichao Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将经典配对二项样本量计算方法（Miettinen, 1968）适配到量化基准测试的保守最小可检测效应（MDE）界，并通过对4个模型和4个基准测试的试点审计及MMLU提示模板研究，展示了该界如何将量化可靠性问题转化为基准设计者可以预先承诺的单行预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化基准测试的可重复性问题日益突出，研究者需要一种方法来判断观察到的量化差异是真实效应还是统计噪声。现有方法缺乏在运行审计前就能承诺的可靠性预算。

Method: 将经典配对二项样本量计算公式适配到量化基准场景，推导出保守MDE界δ* ≤ (z_{1-α/2}+z_{1-β})√(ρ_d/m)；通过4个模型×4个基准（k=5折，n=100）的未配对试点审计进行验证；并行MMLU提示模板研究比较量化噪声与提示噪声的规模；提供五线预注册模板。

Result: 设ρ_d=0.10时，所有NF4-FP16差异均低于MDE界；多数跨折标准差落在二项参考值±1.5pp范围内；OPT-WinoGrande在ρ_d=0.10下低于MDE界，但在ρ_d=0.05下超出；MMLU提示模板范围（2-10pp）达到或超过最大量化差异（3.2pp）。

Conclusion: n=100子样本上报告的大部分"基准不可靠性"方差实际上是二项抽样噪声；量化审计必须先固定提示模板，否则模板方差会混入噪声基底；所提MDE界为基准设计者提供了可操作的规划工具。

Abstract: This is a planning-method note with an unpaired pilot audit. We adapt the classical paired-binary sample-size calculation (Miettinen, 1968) to quantization benchmarks, giving a conservative minimum detectable effect (MDE) bound $δ^{*} \le (z_{1-α/2}+z_{1-β})\sqrt{ρ_d/m}$ in the paired item count $m$ and the FP16-NF4 disagreement rate $ρ_d$. The bound turns "how reliable is my quantization claim?" into a one-line budget a benchmark designer can commit to before running. We illustrate the bound on four models and four benchmarks ($k=5$ splits of $n=100$), and add a parallel MMLU prompt-template study to put the bound's quantization-noise scale alongside the prompt-noise scale. Assuming $ρ_d=0.10$ (an unmeasured planning value), all observed NF4-FP16 deltas fall below the implied MDE, and most cross-split SDs lie within $\pm 1.5$ pp of the binomial reference $\sqrt{p(1-p)/n}$, so much of the variance reported as "benchmark unreliability" on $n=100$ subsamples is binomial sampling noise. The single borderline cell (OPT-WinoGrande, $|Δ|=3.2$ pp) is below the implied MDE at $ρ_d=0.10$ but above it at $ρ_d=0.05$, illustrating the planning trade-off the bound makes explicit. On MMLU, prompt-template ranges of 2-10 pp meet or exceed the largest observed quantization delta (3.2 pp), so a quantization audit that does not first fix the prompt template absorbs template variance into its noise floor. We complement the bound with a five-line pre-registration template.

</details>


### [95] [A Theoretical and Experimental Study of a Novel Adaptive Learning Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29273)
*Sakshi Kumari,Shyam Kumar M,Sushmitha P*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文回顾了Adam和AMSGrad等自适应优化方法，提出基于视线方法的新优化器C-Adam，提供了收敛性理论证明，并通过数值实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应学习率优化器（如Adam）在实际任务中被广泛使用但不保证收敛，AMSGrad虽解决了非收敛问题但仍存在局限性，因此需要设计一种既能保证收敛又能减少计算开销和振荡的新型优化器。

Method: 基于视线方法（line of sight approach）提出C-Adam优化器，在Adam/AMSGrad框架基础上引入新的修正机制，并给出收敛性的理论证明。

Result: 通过多项基于真实数据的数值实验，验证了C-Adam优化器的有效性，表明其在收敛性和计算效率方面优于Adam和AMSGrad。

Conclusion: C-Adam作为一种新型自适应优化器，在保证收敛的同时降低了计算成本和振荡，是对现有自适应优化方法的有效改进。

Abstract: A crucial component of machine learning algorithms is minimizing loss functions with less computational cost and less oscillations. While adaptive learning rate-based optimizers have been widely used for real-world tasks, they do not guarantee convergence, which is why AMSGrad was later introduced to investigate the non-convergence behaviour of Adam. In this paper, popular adaptive optimization methods like Adam and AMSGrad are critically reviewed with an emphasis on their fundamental design concepts. To address limitations of the above mentioned optimizers, a new optimizer variant, C-Adam, is proposed based on the line of sight approach. A theoretical proof for convergence is also provided and the optimizer is validated through a number of real-life based numerical experiments.

</details>


### [96] [Singularity-aware Optimization via Randomized Geometric Probing: Towards Stable Non-smooth Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29547)
*Ruoran Xu,Borong She,Xiaobo Jin,Qiufeng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出S-Adam优化器，通过局部几何不稳定性（LGI）指标动态调整步长，解决非光滑损失景观下Adam优化器的梯度抖动问题，在量化感知训练和高噪声小批量学习中取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习架构（如ReLU激活、量化算子）引入的非光滑组件导致损失景观不光滑，传统Adam等自适应优化器在Clarke次微分区域产生梯度抖动（剧烈振荡），导致收敛性差和泛化性能次优。

Method: 提出S-Adam优化器，核心贡献是局部几何不稳定性（LGI）指标——通过随机方向导数的方差高效估计Clarke次微分直径；并引入自适应阻尼机制exp(-λρ)，在高不稳定区域减速更新，在光滑区域保持快速收敛。

Result: 在CIFAR-100上准确率提升达6%，在TinyImageNet上提升达3%；有效缓解梯度振荡；在量化感知训练和高噪声小批量学习中一致优于AdamW和Prox-SGD。

Conclusion: 通过微分包含理论证明了S-Adam几乎必然收敛到(δ,ε)-Clarke稳定点，且保持最优O(1/√T)收敛速率。

Abstract: Deep learning optimization relies heavily on the assumption of smooth loss landscapes, a condition systematically violated by modern architectures due to non-smooth components such as ReLU activations and quantization operators. In such non-smooth regimes, adaptive optimizers such as Adam suffer from gradient chattering, violent oscillations caused by conflicting signals within the Clarke subdifferential, leading to poor convergence and suboptimal generalization. To address this, we introduce Singularity-aware Adam (S-Adam), a novel optimizer that stabilizes training by dynamically modulating step sizes based on local geometric instability. Our key contribution is the Local Geometric Instability (LGI) metric, a computationally efficient estimator of the Clarke subdifferential diameter derived from the variance of randomized directional derivatives. S-Adam incorporates an adaptive damping mechanism exp(-$λ$$ρ$) that decelerates updates in high-instability regions while preserving fast convergence in smooth basins. We provide a rigorous convergence analysis using differential inclusions, proving that S-Adam converges almost surely to ($δ$,$ε$)-Clarke stationary points at the optimal O(1/$\sqrt(T)$) rate. Empirical evaluations on Quantization-Aware Training (QAT) and high-noise small-batch learning demonstrate that S-Adam consistently outperforms AdamW and Prox-SGD, achieving accuracy gains of up to 6 percent on CIFAR-100 and 3 percent on TinyImageNet while effectively mitigating gradient oscillations.

</details>


### [97] [Context Distillation as Latent Memory Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28889)
*Ziyang Zheng,Zeju Li,Xiangyu Wen,Jianyuan Zhong,Junhua Huang,Lei Chen,Mingxuan Yuan,Qiang Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将上下文蒸馏建模为潜在记忆管理问题，通过将每个上下文蒸馏为独立LoRA适配器形成模块化记忆库，结合检索、路由和自门控机制实现显式记忆选择与安全激活，并引入缓存共享提升推理效率。实验表明该方法显著优于基线，自门控机制通过停用不必要记忆提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文蒸馏方法忽略了多个蒸馏后的潜在记忆应如何存储、检索以及在非理想环境下安全激活的问题。

Method: 将每个上下文蒸馏为独立LoRA适配器形成模块化记忆库；给定查询时检索候选记忆，将查询路由到最合适的适配器，并使用自门控机制决定是否激活潜在记忆；引入缓存共享降低推理开销。

Result: 该方法在检索场景下显著优于基线，自门控机制通过停用不必要的潜在记忆提升了模型鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 将上下文蒸馏重新定义为潜在记忆管理问题，模块化记忆库结合显式选择与自门控机制可有效提升蒸馏记忆的存储、检索和安全激活能力。

Abstract: Context distillation compresses contextual information into model parameters, yet existing methods often ignore how multiple distilled latent memories should be stored, retrieved, and safely activated in non-oracle settings. We formulate context distillation as a latent memory management problem. We distill each context into an independent LoRA adapter, forming a modular memory bank that enables explicit memory selection. Given a query, our framework retrieves candidate memories, routes the query to the most suitable adapter, and uses a Self-Gating mechanism to decide whether latent memory should be activated. To improve efficiency, we further introduce cache sharing to reduce management overhead during inference. Experiments show that our method substantially outperforms baselines with retrieval, while Self-Gating improves robustness by deactivate unnecessary latent memories.

</details>


### [98] [Open Problem: Separating Geometric and Algorithmic Compression via Cayley-Table Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29885)
*Dongsung Huh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出现代深度学习缺乏对算法复杂度的最小化偏置，提出Cayley表补全作为离散代数领域的标准测试床，类比矩阵补全中的隐式几何偏置，并呼吁建立形式化精确恢复界与推广连续平坦性先验以自主发现离散算法公理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习方法在连续域中表现优异，但无法外推到精确的算法规则或离散代数规则，缺乏对算法复杂度最小化的归纳偏置。作者希望填补这一空白，推动离散代数结构的学习理论发展。

Method: 将Cayley表补全作为离散代数的标准测试问题，类比矩阵补全；利用算子值张量分解结合平坦性先验来产生对离散结合律的隐式算法偏置。

Result: 提出待解决的形式化精确恢复界问题；证明了平坦性先验可诱导离散结合律偏置，但尚未建立严格的数学保证。

Conclusion: 呼吁学界推广连续平坦性先验，使其无需组合搜索即可自主发现更广泛的离散算法公理，建立Cayley表补全的理论基础。

Abstract: Modern statistical learning theory and deep learning characterize generalization primarily in terms of continuous capacity control (e.g., norm-based regularization, margin maximization, low-rank bias). While highly successful in continuous domains, deep learning consistently fails to extrapolate exact algorithmic or discrete algebraic rules, reflecting a missing inductive bias toward algorithmic complexity minimization. We propose the Cayley-table completion as the canonical testbed for this missing bias, serving as the discrete algebraic counterpart to matrix completion. Just as matrix factorization combined with weight decay yields an implicit geometric bias toward low linear rank, recent results demonstrate that operator-valued tensor factorizations paired with a flatness prior yield an implicit algorithmic bias toward exact discrete associativity. We pose the open problem of establishing formal exact recovery bounds for Cayley-table completion, and challenge the community to generalize continuous flatness priors to autonomously discover broader discrete algorithmic axioms without combinatorial search.

</details>


### [99] [Feature Geometry of LoRA Adapters: A Sparse Autoencoder Analysis of Representational Divergence in Fine-Tuned Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28896)
*Prasanth K K*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过稀疏自编码器（SAEs）研究LoRA微调引发的表示几何变化，发现LoRA诱导的特征与预训练SAE字典之间存在弱几何对齐，表明LoRA更新在残差流中占据部分独特的表示结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA微调虽被广泛使用，但其对大型语言模型内部表示的具体影响尚不明确，理解这些变化对于可解释性和安全审计至关重要。

Method: 提出delta激活框架隔离LoRA贡献，在Gemma-2-9B上使用秩4/8/16/32训练适配器专用SAEs，通过余弦相似度、主角度分析和CKA评估特征空间对齐。

Result: LoRA特征字典与预训练SAE特征之间几何对齐较弱；适配器专用SAEs能更有效地重建delta激活；特征密度随秩和深度增加而增加，几何分歧在不同秩下保持相对稳定。

Conclusion: LoRA微调可诱导出预训练可解释性字典无法完全捕获的特征结构，这对机制可解释性、适应性分析及微调语言模型的安全审计具有启示意义。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) has emerged as a widely adopted approach for adapting large language models, yet the internal representational changes induced by LoRA fine-tuning remain insufficiently understood. In this work, we investigate the geometry of LoRA-induced representations using Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs). We introduce a delta activation framework that isolates the adapter-specific contribution to the residual stream.
  Using Gemma-2-9B with LoRA ranks 4, 8, 16, and 32, we train adapter-specific SAEs across multiple transformer layers and compare their learned feature spaces with pretrained SAE dictionaries. We evaluate representational alignment using cosine similarity between decoder directions, principal-angle analysis of feature subspaces, and Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA) between activation representations.
  Across layers and ranks, we consistently observe comparatively weak geometric alignment between LoRA-induced feature dictionaries and pretrained SAE features. Adapter-specific SAEs also reconstruct delta activations more effectively than pretrained SAEs, suggesting that LoRA updates occupy partially distinct representational structure within the residual stream. Additionally, feature density increases with rank and depth, while geometric divergence remains relatively stable across ranks.
  These findings provide empirical evidence that LoRA fine-tuning can induce feature structures that are not fully captured by pretrained interpretability dictionaries, with implications for mechanistic interpretability, adaptation analysis, and safety auditing of fine-tuned language models.

</details>


### [100] [Spectral Guidance for Flexible and Efficient Control of Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28900)
*Gabriel Moreira,Manuel Marques,João Paulo Costeira,Chenyan Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Spectral Guidance框架，利用生成过程的几何结构，通过学习条件期望算子的奇异函数，将任意引导信号（标签、CLIP嵌入、掩码等）直接投影到采样轨迹上，无需重训练或反向传播。在CIFAR-10上相比最强无训练基线提升37个百分点的条件准确率，采样速度提升4倍，并揭示了引导的最佳时间窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型控制方法需要重训练或反向传播，计算成本高且不稳定。作者希望利用生成过程的固有几何结构，实现高效、稳定且无需额外训练的通用引导框架。

Method: 1) 将噪声数据中仍保持信息性的特征定义为条件期望算子的奇异函数；2) 通过自监督目标学习该基函数；3) 将任意引导信号（标签、CLIP嵌入、掩码等）投影到该基上，直接融入采样轨迹。

Result: CIFAR-10上条件准确率相比最强无训练基线提升37个百分点，采样速度提升4倍；同一套表示同时支持标签、CLIP和空间掩码引导，无需辅助模型。

Conclusion: 该框架揭示了生成过程中的相变现象，可精确定位有效引导的最优时间窗口，为扩散模型的控制提供了理论支撑和实用方法。

Abstract: We introduce Spectral Guidance, a framework for controlling diffusion models by leveraging the intrinsic geometry of the generative process. As data is progressively corrupted by noise, only a small number of features remain informative for control. We characterize them as the singular functions of a conditional expectation operator and show that they can be learned via a self-supervised objective. Once recovered, this basis enables the projection of arbitrary guidance signals, such as labels, CLIP embeddings, or masks, directly onto the sampling trajectory. This approach allows for stable, high-fidelity control without retraining or denoiser backpropagation during sampling. Empirically, we improve conditional accuracy on CIFAR-10 by 37 percentage points over the strongest training-free baseline while offering $4\times$ faster sampling. Moreover, the same representations that support label and CLIP guidance also enable spatial control, such as mask-based guidance, without auxiliary models. Finally, our framework reveals a phase transition in the generative process, pinpointing the optimal time window for effective guidance.

</details>


### [101] [When LLM Reward Design Fails: Diagnostic-Driven Refinement for Sparse Structured RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28918)
*Youting Wang,Yuan Tang,Bowen Liu,Xuan Liu,Dingyan Shang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将LLM生成奖励塑形函数视为调试而非一次性生成，通过诊断驱动的迭代优化，在稀疏结构化任务中显著提升PPO训练效果，但在密集奖励的连续控制场景中存在边界限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM生成奖励函数的一次性方法存在奖励泛滥、语义/API误解等失败模式，需要系统性的调试框架来改进。

Method: 提出诊断驱动的迭代优化方法：基于训练诊断指标和失败模式分类法指导奖励函数修正。采用MiniGrid为主评估环境，MuJoCo为边界压力测试，PPO作为训练算法。

Result: DoorKey-8x8从2.3%提升至97.6%，KeyCorridor从31.2%提升至86.7%；消融实验证明分类法提示是主要机制；静态词汇控制恢复大部分差距；预算匹配和Best-of-3控制排除了选择效应和训练时间效应。

Conclusion: 该方法适用于具有可靠接口的PPO稀疏结构化任务，在密集奖励连续控制场景中效果有限；分类法提示是主要贡献机制，动态标签提供部分增量证据。

Abstract: For sparse, structured reinforcement-learning tasks with semantic reward-function interfaces, LLM-generated reward shaping is better framed as debugging than one-shot generation. We study PPO-trained agents using MiniGrid as core evaluation and MuJoCo as boundary stress test. Our audit finds two dominant one-shot failure modes -- reward flooding and semantic/API misunderstanding -- plus a rarer weak-shaping case. We propose diagnostic-driven iterative refinement, where training diagnostics and a failure-mode taxonomy guide targeted reward-function revision. Refinement improves DoorKey-8x8 from 2.3% to 97.6% and KeyCorridor from 31.2% to 86.7% with high seed-to-seed variance. Controls show these gains are not from retrying or extra training: metrics-only re-prompting yields large drops, while a static-vocabulary control recovers much of the gap (87.6%; 70.7%), showing the taxonomy prompt is a major mechanism and dynamic labels provide only partially isolated incremental evidence. Budget-matched and Best-of-3 comparisons separate refinement from selection and training-time effects. Component-removal tests, sensitivity analyses, and an audit against author labels provide converging evidence for the debugging interpretation while revealing calibration limits. Continuous-control results show the boundary: success-based diagnostics can misfire in dense-reward locomotion, and return-trend feedback removes one false-positive mechanism without robust gains. The low-call protocol is a cost contrast with population-based reward search, not a benchmark comparison. In four crossed-variance-design environments, point estimates suggest larger gains when LLM reward-function variance dominates but bootstrap intervals are wide. The method is bounded to sparse structured tasks with reliable interfaces under PPO; fields like event_text may help, hurt, or be neutral.

</details>


### [102] [CosmicFish-HRM: Adaptive Reasoning via Hierarchical Recurrent Mechanisms in Compact Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28919)
*Venkat Akhil Lakkapragada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CosmicFish-HRM，一个紧凑型语言模型，通过分层推理模块（HRM）动态分配推理深度，而非单纯扩大参数规模。模型根据输入复杂度自适应调整高低层级推理循环次数。尽管小规模时推理基础设施带来开销，但随模型增大该代价相对降低，有望成为提升推理能力的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型虽推理能力强，但参数量巨大、推理成本高昂。本文探索另一方向：在紧凑模型中实现自适应推理深度，以更高效的方式提升推理能力，避免单纯依赖参数规模扩张。

Method: 提出了分层推理模块（HRM），动态分配推理计算量。模型通过高低层级推理循环迭代，学习根据输入复杂度自动决定何时停止推理。结合了现代Transformer组件（Grouped Query Attention、RoPE、SwiGLU激活函数）。

Result: 模型学会了非均匀的推理行为，能根据不同任务和输入分配不同数量的推理步骤。小规模时推理基础设施带来额外开销，但假设随模型规模增大HRM核心的相对成本会降低。

Conclusion: 自适应推理深度可能为仅依赖参数规模来提升推理能力提供一种有前景的替代方案。

Abstract: Large language models have achieved strong reasoning capabilities, though often at the cost of massive parameter counts and expensive inference. In this work, we explore a different direction: adaptive reasoning depth in compact language models. We present CosmicFish-HRM, a compact language model built around a Hierarchical Reasoning Module (HRM) that dynamically allocates computational effort during inference. Instead of applying fixed computation to every input, the model iterates through high-level and low-level reasoning cycles and learns when to halt based on input complexity. CosmicFish-HRM combines this adaptive reasoning core with modern transformer components including Grouped Query Attention, RoPE, and SwiGLU activations. While the additional reasoning infrastructure introduces overhead at small scale, we hypothesize that this tradeoff becomes increasingly favorable as model size grows and the relative cost of the HRM core diminishes. Our results show that the model learns non-uniform reasoning behavior, allocating different numbers of reasoning steps across tasks and inputs. These findings suggest that adaptive reasoning depth may offer a promising alternative to relying solely on parameter scale for reasoning capability.

</details>


### [103] [A Training-Time Diagnostic for Generalization via the Log-Alignment Ratio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28975)
*Ali Shehper,Ashish Vaswani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新定义了log对齐比(LAR)为权重谱与激活谱之间的重叠度量，并证明LAR能追踪记忆与泛化之间的转换：在grokking中预测学习函数的有效维度 ($k \approx n^{2(1-LAR)}$)，在3B参数语言模型预训练中通过与无过拟合基线的偏差追踪泛化差距。LAR可通过前向传播计算，无需验证数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数化理论中提出的log对齐比(LAR)需要更清晰的理论解释和实际应用验证，作者旨在为LAR提供谱理论的重新表述，并探索其作为记忆-泛化转换指标的能力。

Method: 将LAR重新表述为权重谱$p$（矩阵归一化平方奇异值的分布）与激活谱$q$（输入在奇异方向上的归一化平方投影分布）之间的重叠度量；在grokking和3B参数语言模型预训练两个场景中实证验证LAR的行为。

Result: 在grokking中，LAR预测学习函数的有效维度$k \approx n^{2(1-LAR)}$；在3B参数语言模型预训练中，LAR与无过拟合基线的偏差追踪泛化差距，且其下降速率在过拟合接近时增加。

Conclusion: LAR是一个计算高效、无需验证数据的有效指标，能够捕捉模型从记忆到泛化的转变，并预测泛化性能。

Abstract: We study the log-alignment ratio (LAR), a measure of parameter-activation alignment, introduced in parameterization theory. We reformulate it as the overlap between a weight spectrum $p$ of the normalized squared singular values of a matrix and an activation spectrum $q$ of the normalized squared projections of inputs onto its singular directions. We show that unembedding LAR tracks the transition between memorization and generalization in two different settings by capturing the spread of $p$ and $q$ during training. In grokking, LAR predicts the effective dimension of the learned function: $k \approx n^{2(1-\text{LAR})}$, where $n$ is the input dimension of the matrix. In 3B-parameter language model pre-training, its deviation from a non-overfitting baseline tracks the generalization gap, and its rate of decline increases as overfitting approaches. LAR is computable from quantities available during the forward pass with negligible computational overhead, and requires no held-out validation data.

</details>


### [104] [Comparing Post-Hoc Explainable AI Methods for Interpreting Black-Box EEG Models in Depression Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28977)
*Antonia Šarčević,Nikolina Frid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多种后验可解释性方法（DeepSHAP、Integrated Gradients、GradCAM、Occlusion、Permutation Feature Importance）在基于InceptionTime架构的EEG抑郁症分类中的应用，发现不同方法在额叶、颞叶、后部及右半球区域呈现出部分收敛的归因模式，但也存在显著的方法学差异，强调后验可解释性在精神病学EEG黑箱分类器中的用途与局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在EEG抑郁症检测中表现优异，但高容量模型的决策过程难以解释，亟需探究不同可解释性方法在该场景下的表现与一致性。

Method: 采用InceptionTime架构进行EEG-MDD分类，结合Shapley法（DeepSHAP）、梯度法（Integrated Gradients、GradCAM）和扰动法（Occlusion、Permutation Feature Importance）五种后验归因方法，在受试者级分层5折交叉验证框架下进行全局归因聚合分析。

Result: 不同方法在额叶、颞叶、后部及右半球区域呈现部分一致的归因模式；梯度法与扰动法之间一致性较高，DeepSHAP则产生明显不同的归因分布；方法间的变异性凸显了假设差异对解释结果的影响。

Conclusion: 不同后验可解释性方法捕捉到部分重叠的EEG抑郁症检测相关性结构，与既往EEG-MDD研究总体一致，但分析应视为探索性而非确定性神经生理生物标志物或临床适用性证据，需谨慎对待方法学差异带来的解释不确定性。

Abstract: Recent advances in deep learning have enabled increasingly accurate electroencephalography (EEG)-based classification of Major Depressive Disorder (MDD), but the decision-making processes of high-capacity models remain difficult to interpret. This study investigates multiple post-hoc explainability methods applied to an InceptionTime architecture trained for EEG-based MDD detection. The analysis includes Shapley-based, gradient-based, and perturbation-based attribution approaches: DeepSHAP, Integrated Gradients, GradCAM, Occlusion, and Permutation Feature Importance. Explainability analysis was performed within a subject-level stratified 5-fold cross-validation framework using global attribution aggregation across EEG segments and subjects. The evaluated methods revealed partially convergent attribution patterns, with recurring emphasis on frontal, temporal, and posterior EEG regions, particularly in the right hemisphere. Quantitative comparison demonstrated substantial agreement between gradient- and perturbation-based approaches, while DeepSHAP produced comparatively distinct attribution distributions. At the same time, variability between explainability methods highlighted the influence of methodological assumptions on the resulting explanations. Overall, the results suggest that different post-hoc explainability approaches capture partially overlapping relevance structures in EEG-based deep learning models for depression detection. Although the observed attribution patterns are broadly consistent with several previous EEG studies of MDD, the analysis should be interpreted as exploratory rather than evidence of definitive neurophysiological biomarkers or clinical applicability. The study highlights both the usefulness and limitations of post-hoc explainability for interpreting black-box EEG classifiers in psychiatric applications.

</details>


### [105] [Learning Robust and Task-Invariant Functional Representation from fMRI through Siamese Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28990)
*Jiyao Wang,Peiyu Duan,Nicha C. Dvornek,Lawrence H. Staib,Denis Sukhodolsky,Pamela Ventola,James S. Duncan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BrainSimSiam是一种轻量级自监督表示学习框架，利用正样本对从fMRI数据中学习鲁棒且可泛化的特征，在数据有限的情况下，其性能优于全监督基线，接近大规模模型的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: fMRI数据获取成本高、精神科评定量表主观性强，导致小样本量和标签质量不一，加上fMRI高维度特性，模型过拟合风险大。现有fMRI基础模型虽然结合多数据集，但预训练和微调的计算资源要求过高。

Method: 提出BrainSimSiam，一种轻量级自监督表示学习框架，仅利用正样本数据对进行对比学习，无需负样本或大规模批量。

Result: 学到的表示在多个下游分类和回归任务上优于全监督基线，接近大规模模型的性能，展现了优异的数据效率和泛化能力。

Conclusion: BrainSimSiam为数据受限的神经影像应用提供了高效可行的解决方案，验证了轻量级自监督方法在fMRI表示学习中的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) is a powerful tool for investigating human brain function. However, the high cost of data acquisition and the inherent subjectivity of psychiatric rating scales often lead to datasets with small sample sizes and variable label quality, especially when targeting a specific neurological condition. Combined with the inherently high dimensionality of fMRI data, these limitations substantially increase the risk of model overfitting. Recent years have seen growing interest in developing fMRI foundation models by combining multiple datasets; however, the computational resources needed for pretraining and fine-tuning are often prohibitive. We show that a lightweight self-supervised framework yields representations that generalize across diverse downstream tasks, outperforming fully supervised baselines and approaching the performance of large-scale models. We introduce BrainSimSiam, a data-efficient self-supervised representation learning framework that leverages positive-only data pairs to learn robust and generalizable features. We demonstrate that the learned representations achieve strong performance across multiple downstream classification and regression tasks, highlighting the potential of BrainSimSiam for data-limited neuroimaging applications.

</details>


### [106] [FormInv: A Measurement Protocol for Semantic Invariance in Mathematical Reasoning Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29001)
*Nishal Thomas,Noel Thomas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现 MathCheck (ICLR 2025) 数据集中 3.1% 的改写存在语义错误，修正后 GPT-4o 排名从第 2 降至第 4；进一步揭示标准基准无法捕捉的语义一致性缺口（SCR 跨度 50-82% 远超准确率 86-96%），并提出 No-Free-Benchmark 推论——通过加权不同改写族可实现任意目标排名。作者发布 FormInv 审计协议与评估工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试仅依赖单模型评估或聚合准确率，无法检测改写语义错误对模型排名的系统性影响；排名结果可能因改写族选择而产生偏差，需建立形式化的语义不变性审计方法。

Method: 对 MathCheck 129 组改写进行跨模型一致性审计（≥3/4 模型一致性判定错误），并在自有数据集（366-811 条 Lean4 验证定理）上扩展至 9 个前沿模型，提出 SCR（语义一致性率）和每定理 Cochran's Q 作为不变性度量，并基于 No-Free-Benchmark 推论推导加权改写族可达成任意排名。

Result: MathCheck 中 3.1% 的改写语义错误导致 GPT-4o 排名下降；自有数据集中 47% 的连接词变体改写存在语义错误；9 模型准确率仅差 86-96%（10 点），SCR 却差 50-82%（32 点）；存在加权改写族可实现任意目标排名。

Conclusion: 标准基准无法反映语义不变性这一关键维度，模型排名高度依赖改写族选择；FormInv 协议可低成本（<10 美元）自动化检测语义错误并提供更鲁棒的模型选择指南。

Abstract: A paraphrase-quality audit of MathCheck (ICLR 2025) detected 4 semantically incorrect paraphrases in 129 groups (3.1%); removing them drops GPT-4o from rank 2 to rank 4 and elevates Claude Haiku and DeepSeek V3 above it; these ranking changes are invisible to any single-model evaluation. Cross-model unanimity found these errors automatically (>= 3/4 models for MathCheck; >= 6/9 for our primary evaluation) for under $10; in our own dataset the same protocol found that 47% of auto-generated connective-variation paraphrases were semantically incorrect. That flaw compounds a deeper measurement gap: Claude Haiku 4.5 achieves 86% accuracy yet SCR=50%, meaning half its theorems are answered differently under semantically equivalent restatements, while aggregate accuracy across 9 models spans only 86-96% yet Semantic Consistency Rates (SCR) span 50-82% -- a 32-point gap invisible to standard benchmarks. Formally, for any target ranking over 9 frontier models there exists a weighting over paraphrase families that realizes it (No-Free-Benchmark corollary), because no model Pareto-dominates all families -- so benchmark designers who select families are implicitly choosing which model wins. FormInv supplies the audit protocol (replicated on external benchmarks at 100% recall), SCR and per-theorem Cochran's Q as primary invariance measures evaluated on 9 models across 366-811 items (on Lean4-verified theorems), and FormInvSelector for regime-aware model selection.

</details>


### [107] [LoRe: Adaptive Interaction-Evaluation Routing with Per-Step Interaction Budgets for Iterative Graph Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29005)
*Jintao Li,Yong-Yi Wang,Zheng-An Wang,Heng Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LoRe是一个无训练、推理时即插即用的包装器，通过动态路由计算到高冲突/高不确定性的交互上，在每次迭代中只评估固定比例的交互，从而显著提升扩散组合优化求解器的可扩展性，在MIS问题上实现超过基线3倍的推理规模、约8倍加速和约12倍峰值内存降低，在TSP上实现约15倍加速和44倍内存降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于扩散的组合优化神经求解器在每次迭代中反复评估密集的边/因子交互，导致推理在墙钟时间上昂贵且在大规模场景下受内存瓶颈限制。

Method: 受多体物理计算方法的启发，LoRe在每个迭代中只评估固定比例的交互，通过动态路由计算到高冲突或高不确定性的交互上，而非使用固定的稀疏化方案（如静态kNN图或静态掩码）。

Result: 在MIS问题上：推理规模超过基线3倍（突破内存极限），约8倍加速，约12倍峰值内存降低，解质量保持。在TSP上（n=1000）：约15倍加速，44倍内存降低，竞争性的路径质量。同时展现出对拓扑 shifts 的零样本鲁棒性。

Conclusion: LoRe作为无训练、推理时的即插即用包装器，通过动态交互预算分配有效解决了扩散组合优化求解器的可扩展性问题，在多个任务上实现了显著的加速和内存节省，同时保持解质量。

Abstract: Diffusion-based neural solvers for combinatorial optimization repeatedly re-evaluate dense edge/factor interactions, making inference expensive in wall-clock time and often memory-bound at scale. Inspired by the computational methodologies of many-body physics, we introduce LoRe, a training-free, inference-time drop-in wrapper that enforces per-step interaction-evaluation budgeting: at each iteration, it evaluates only a fixed fraction of interactions by dynamically routing computation to high-conflict or high-uncertainty interactions, instead of using a fixed sparsification (e.g., static kNN graphs or static masks). Under fully inclusive end-to-end wall-clock accounting, LoRe substantially improves scalability on the Maximum Independent Set (MIS) problem, extending feasible inference more than $3\times$ beyond the baseline's out-of-memory limit, delivering a $\sim 8\times$ speedup and a $\sim 12\times$ peak-memory reduction, with solution quality preserved in this regime. Demonstrating cross-task generality on the large-scale Traveling Salesperson Problem (TSP) and zero-shot robustness to topology shifts, LoRe achieves a $\sim 15\times$ speedup at $n=1000$ with a $44\times$ memory reduction and competitive tour quality.

</details>


### [108] [Causal Intelligence for Constraint-Aware Intervention Design to Induce State Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29008)
*Zixuan Song,Uwe Mueller,Dimitris V. Manatakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: COAST是一种基于因果智能的方法，用于在计算机上设计约束性干预措施，诱导系统实现用户定义的状态转换，通过整合因果图学习、机制级驱动因子归因和约束感知多目标优化，在合成数据和真实生物数据上有效识别关键因果驱动因子及稳健的干预策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测模型缺乏机制性洞察和用于决策的因果框架，无法系统性地设计从一种状态到另一种状态的靶向干预方案。

Method: COAST包含四个模块：特征选择、因果发现（学习上下文特定的因果图）、因果建模（构建结构因果模型）、干预识别与评估（提出约束感知的多目标优化公式，平衡转换效能、干预复杂度和目标状态稳定性）。

Result: 在合成基准和真实生物数据集上，COAST成功恢复了关键因果驱动因子，并识别出稳健的单目标和多目标干预策略，实现了期望的状态转换。

Conclusion: COAST提供了一种模块化、领域无关的因果智能框架，能够生成透明且可指导实验验证的机制性解释，为状态转换干预设计提供了原则性决策框架。

Abstract: Driving a system from one state to another through targeted interventions is a fundamental challenge in science, yet most predictive models offer limited mechanistic insight and no principled framework for decision-making. Here we present COAST (Causally Optimal Actions for State Transitions), a causal-intelligence approach for the in-silico design of constrained interventions that induce user-defined state transitions. Given data characterizing source and target states, COAST learns context-specific causal graphs and structural causal models, attributes observed distributional shifts to mechanism-level causal drivers, and introduces a novel constraint-aware multi-objective optimization formulation that balances transition efficacy, intervention complexity, and target-state stability. The approach is modular and domain-agnostic, integrating feature selection, causal discovery, causal modeling, and intervention identification and evaluation through interchangeable components. Across synthetic benchmarks and real biological datasets, COAST recovers key causal drivers and identifies robust single- and multi-target intervention strategies that achieve desired state transitions, accompanied by transparent mechanistic rationales to guide experimental validation.

</details>


### [109] [Theoretical Foundations and Effective Algorithms for Policy-Aware Simulator Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29032)
*Christoph Dann,Yishay Mansour,Mehryar Mohri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出以战略鲁棒性而非预测精度作为世界模型的学习目标，通过零和极小极大博弈框架，结合理论保证（可学习性、局部 critic 损失界、误差-MDP对偶性），在连续控制任务中实现策略从仿真到真实世界的成功迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于模型的强化学习智能体通常通过最小化预测损失来学习世界模型，但强大的RL优化器会利用模型的微小不准确性，导致模拟器利用和现实差距——策略在仿真中成功但在真实世界中失败。

Method: 将学习模拟器的目标从预测精度转为战略鲁棒性，形式化为模型玩家与对抗策略玩家之间的零和极小极大博弈；提供三个理论分析：在线学习保证（亚线性遗憾界）、基于critic的简化（用局部critic损失界全局策略价值差距）、误差-MDP对偶性（最坏情况策略等价于以单步critic误差为奖励的标准RL问题）。

Result: 在连续控制任务上，该方法将战略重要区域的预测误差降低1.5-2.2倍，并使纯仿真训练的策略达到接近最优的真实世界性能。

Conclusion: 以战略鲁棒性为目标学习世界模型，结合极小极大博弈框架，能够有效缩小仿真到真实的差距，实现策略从仿真到真实世界的成功迁移。

Abstract: Model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) agents typically learn world models by minimizing predictive loss. However, powerful RL optimizers inevitably exploit minor model inaccuracies, leading to simulator exploitation and a reality gap where policies succeed in simulation but fail in the real world. We propose that the objective for learning simulators should be strategic robustness rather than predictive accuracy, and formulate this as a zero-sum minimax game between a model player and an adversarial policy player. We provide a comprehensive theoretical analysis: (1) an online learning guarantee showing the game is learnable with sublinear regret bounds; (2) a tractable critic-based simplification bounding the global policy-value gap by the local critic's loss; and (3) an Error-MDP duality, proving that finding the worst-case policy is formally dual to a standard RL problem where the reward is the one-step critic error. This duality yields a provably convergent active data selection algorithm. Experiments on continuous control tasks demonstrate that our approach reduces prediction error in strategically important regions by $1.5$-$2.2\times$ and enables policies trained purely in simulation to match near-optimal real-world performance.

</details>


### [110] [Moment Matching Q-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29033)
*Yiyan,Liang,Sifei Liu,Weitong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Moment Matching Q-Learning (MoMa QL)框架，利用最大均值差异（MMD）技术匹配原始分布与目标分布的所有阶统计量，加速基于分数和流的生成模型在强化学习中的推理过程，在D4RL任务中实现更高计算效率，并在离线到在线强化学习任务中展现出更优的适应性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于分数和流的生成模型在强化学习中推理延迟过长，导致迭代采样的计算瓶颈显著。

Method: 提出MoMa QL框架，利用统计假设检验中的最大均值差异（MMD）技术，匹配原始分布与目标分布的所有阶统计量，对条件得分函数施加分布级别的强正则化。

Result: 在D4RL任务中计算效率更高且性能可比；在离线到在线强化学习任务中，通过加速流策略的动作采样过程，展现出更优的在线交互微调适应性。

Conclusion: MoMa QL通过MMD矩匹配正则化实现了分布级收敛，在不同超参数下保持稳定，显著提升了生成模型在强化学习中的推理效率。

Abstract: Score-based and flow-based generative models exhibit remarkable expressive capacity in capturing complex distributions, and have been extensively deployed in tasks ranging from image generation to reinforcement learning. Nevertheless, these models suffer from prolonged inference latency, which imposes a significant computational bottleneck in RL with iterative sampling. To overcome this limitation, we propose a new framework named Moment Matching Q-Learning (MoMa QL), which utilizes a technique from statistical hypothesis testing known as maximum mean discrepancy (MMD) that intend to match all orders of statistics between the original and target distribution. By enforcing strong regularization on all moment statistics, this algorithm guarantees distribution-level convergence for conditional score function and remains stable under various hyperparameters. Empirically, we show that our method MoMa QL is more computationally efficient with a comparable if not competitive performance in various D4RL tasks. Remarkably, by accelerating the action sampling process for flow-based policies, MoMa QL demonstrates superior performance in offline-to-online RL tasks because of faster and stronger adaptability for online interactive finetuning.

</details>


### [111] [Model Merging by Output-Space Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29101)
*Bethan Evans,Benjamin Etheridge,Stephen Roberts,Jared Tanner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将模型合并形式化为一个凸二次规划问题，通过校准输入和微调模型输出最小化平方输出校准目标，统一了现有启发式合并方法，并提供了预测合并质量的闭合形式诊断指标，实验表明在单层和多层设置中均能匹配或超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型合并方法（如任务算术、模型汤、TIES、DARE）虽然计算高效且经验成功，但依赖启发式设计选择，缺乏形式化的最优性保证。本文旨在为模型合并提供一种有理论保障的优化框架。

Method: 将模型合并表述为关于残差更新的凸二次规划（QP）问题，通过校准输入和微调模型输出来最小化平方输出校准目标；推导出闭合形式的诊断指标（残差能量被选定基捕获的比例）用于预测合并质量；在单层设置中比较QP与现有方法，并扩展到多层合并的逐层顺序算法。

Result: 在单层设置中，QP匹配或超越现有方法；当最优基比廉价的对角QP有显著增益时，本文进行了刻画；在语言和视觉基准上，多层合并的逐层算法持续优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 二次规划框架为模型合并提供了理论化的最优性基础，统一了现有启发式方法，并且诊断指标能有效预测合并质量，多层扩展也展现出稳定的性能提升。

Abstract: Model merging combines fine-tuned checkpoints into a single multi-task model without retraining. Existing methods - such as task arithmetic, model soups, TIES, and DARE - are computationally efficient and empirically successful, but rely on heuristic design choices and lack formal optimality guarantees. We show that merging can be formulated as a convex quadratic programme over residual updates, yielding weights that minimise a squared-output calibration objective using calibration inputs and fine-tuned model outputs, and subsuming existing methods as special cases. Our framework yields a closed-form diagnostic - the fraction of residual energy captured by a chosen basis - that predicts downstream merge quality using only the calibration set. Empirically, the QP matches or outperforms existing methods in the single-layer setting, and we characterise when the optimal basis provides significant gains over the cheaper diagonal QP. We extend to multi-layer merging via a sequential layer-wise algorithm and demonstrate consistent gains across language and vision benchmarks.

</details>


### [112] [When and How Long? The Readout-Mediator Angle in Temporal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29126)
*Shreyas Fadnavis,Praitayini Kanakaraj,Felix Wyss*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现语言模型中线性探针（linear probe）可以完美解码某一表示，但该表示方向与模型实际使用的因果计算方向正交（orthogonal）。通过日历日期时长推理任务，作者证明探针恢复的“一年中的第几天”方向与模型真实使用的四维子空间几乎垂直（角度与随机子空间无异），且 ablation 实验证实探针对模型行为无影响。电路逆向工程揭示了注意力头通过 ±30 和 ±61 天的 QK 偏移路由月份粒度信息，MLP 将绝对日期转换为时长。该解离现象在 1.5B-9B 四个规模、两个模型家族上复制，并在空间位移和符号算术领域有初步证据，表明“读出-中介正交性”（readout-mediator orthogonality）是探针可解释性的一般性失效模式，直接质疑了将探针用作运行时安全监控的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示线性探针可解释性方法的根本缺陷：探针能高精度解码某一特征，但该特征方向可能与模型实际用于推理的因果子空间正交（orthogonal），导致探针完全无法洞察模型内部计算过程。这一失效模式直接威胁以探针为基础的安全监控方案。

Method: 以日历日期时长推理（calendar-date duration reasoning）为测试平台，同时使用线性探针（sin/cos probe 恢复 day-of-year）和 Distributed Alignment Search (DAS) 定位因果子空间；通过测量“读出-中介角”（readout-mediator angle）并与 Haar 均匀随机子空间零分布比较，量化正交性；结合电路逆向工程（circuit reverse-engineering）分析注意力头 QK 偏移和 MLP 转换机制；利用稀疏自编码器（sparse autoencoder）分解验证语义分离。

Result: 探针恢复的子空间与 DAS 定位的因果子空间之间的角度与随机子空间之间的角度无法区分（即正交）；ablating 探针方向对模型答案无影响，而 ablating DAS 四维子空间则完全破坏性能；电路分析揭示注意力头通过学习 ±30 和 ±61 天的 QK 偏移传递月份粒度上下文，MLP 将绝对日期转换为时长，均发生在探针未触及的因果子空间下游；稀疏自编码器证实探针对齐和 DAS 对齐的特征编码语义不重叠的概念；该结果在 1.5B-9B 四个规模、两个模型家族上复制，并在空间位移和符号算术领域有初步证据。

Conclusion: 读出-中介正交性（readout-mediator orthogonality）是探针可解释性的一般性失效模式：探针可能解码一个与模型实际因果计算完全正交的方向，即使探测准确率极高，也无法反映模型内部机制。这直接否定将探针作为运行时安全监控的可行性——探针可能报告高置信度，而模型已悄然放弃该方向的计算。

Abstract: A linear probe can decode a representation almost perfectly and yet be completely irrelevant to how the model uses it. On calendar-date duration reasoning in language models, a $\sin$/$\cos$ probe recovers day-of-year from a layer's activations, yet ablating its direction has no effect on the model's answers -- while ablating a four-dimensional subspace found by Distributed Alignment Search (DAS) at the same layer collapses performance entirely. We measure the angle between these two subspaces -- the \emph{readout-mediator angle} -- and find it indistinguishable from the angle between two random subspaces (the Haar-uniform null), meaning the probe has learned a direction orthogonal to the model's actual computation. Reverse-engineering the circuit reveals why: attention heads route month-grained context through learned QK offsets at ${\pm}30$ and ${\pm}61$ days, and MLPs then convert \emph{when} (absolute date) into \emph{how long} (duration) -- all downstream of the causal subspace the probe never touches. Sparse-autoencoder decomposition confirms the split: probe-aligned and DAS-aligned features encode semantically disjoint concepts with negligible causal overlap. The dissociation replicates across four scales ($1.5$-$9\,$B) and two model families, with preliminary evidence on two further domains (spatial displacement, symbolic arithmetic), suggesting that readout-mediator orthogonality is a general failure mode of probe-based interpretability. This directly undermines proposals to deploy probes as runtime safety monitors: the probe can report high confidence on a direction the model has silently abandoned.

</details>


### [113] [Apertus LLM Family Expansion via Distillation and Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29128)
*Andrei Panferov,Davit Melikidze,Martin Jaggi,Dan Alistarh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文验证了蒸馏和量化是扩展现有模型族至新规模和新硬件格式的经济有效方法，基于Apertus 8B LLM通过蒸馏和量化技术构建了Apertus-v1.1模型族（最高4B参数，在1.7T许可令牌上训练），在覆盖广泛硬件和系统需求方面展现了成本效益和强准确性表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs被广泛应用于各类场景（如聊天助手、数据标注），需要模型满足不同的预算和硬件约束。当前趋势是模型以不同尺寸批量发布以覆盖尽可能广泛的约束范围，但如何低成本地扩展模型族至新规模和新硬件格式仍是问题。

Method: 基于开源配方的Apertus 8B LLM，采用蒸馏和量化技术，在1.7T许可令牌上训练得到参数规模达4B的Apertus-v1.1模型族。

Result: 该方法在覆盖大范围硬件和系统需求方面展现出成本效益和强大的准确性表现。

Conclusion: 蒸馏和量化是扩展模型族至新规模和新硬件格式的经济有效途径，能够在满足多样化部署约束的同时保持良好性能。

Abstract: The wide adoption of LLMs has led to their use in great variety of applications and scenarios, such as chatbot assistants and data annotation, creating the need for the models to satisfy certain budget and hardware constraints. This has led to the trend of LLMs being released in batches consisting of similar models of various sizes for the family of models to adhere to as wide of a range of constraints as possible.
