In this paper, we validate distillation and quantization as a cost-effective way to expand model families to new sizes and hardware formats. Based on the open-recipe Apertus 8B LLM, we produce Apertus-v1.1 - a distilled family of models with up to 4B parameters trained on 1.7T permissive license tokens. We demonstrate cost-efficiency and strong accuracy performance of our approach for covering large ranges of hardware and systems requirements.

</details>


### [114] [Optimal Gap-Dependent Regret for Private Stochastic Decision-Theoretic Online Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29148)
*Tommaso Cesari,Roberto Colomboni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对COLT公开问题，给出了随机决策理论在线学习中在纯事件级差分隐私下的最优间隙相关遗憾界，提出了一个无水平线的纯差分隐私算法，遗憾界为O(log K/Δ_min + log K/ε)，通过分块指数增长、单动作执行、指数机制选择下一动作以及熵势论证实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Hu和Mehta在COLT提出的公开问题：确定随机决策理论在线学习在纯事件级差分隐私下的最优间隙相关遗憾率。

Method: 将时间划分为指数增长的数据块，每个块内执行单一动作；通过指数机制（应用于前一个块的数据独立随机前缀）选择下一块的动作；利用随机前缀将块遗憾转化为所有前缀长度上softmax选择误差之和；使用单个熵势论证控制所有隐私主导的大间隙动作，代价为log K/ε。

Result: 给出了显式遗憾界Reg_T ≤ 1000·(log K/Δ_min + log K/ε)，对所有水平线T成立。该界达到了已知下界O(log K/min{Δ_min, ε})，即最优遗憾率。

Conclusion: 提出的纯差分隐私算法达到了随机决策理论在线学习在纯事件级差分隐私下的最优间隙相关遗憾界，解决了该公开问题。

Abstract: We study stochastic decision-theoretic online learning with full information and event-level pure differential privacy. A COLT open problem of Hu and Mehta asks to determine the optimal gap-dependent regret rate for stochastic decision-theoretic online learning under pure event-level differential privacy. For $K$ actions, losses in $[0,1]$, and a unique best action separated from the second-best action by gap $Δ_{\min}$, the known lower bound is of order $
  \frac{\log K}{\min\{Δ_{\min},\varepsilon\}}, $
or equivalently, up to universal constants, of order \[
  \frac{\log K}{Δ_{\min}}+\frac{\log K}{\varepsilon}. \] We give a horizon-free pure-DP algorithm and prove the explicit regret bound \[
  \operatorname{Reg}_T
  \le
  1000 \cdot \left(\frac{\log K}{Δ_{\min}}+\frac{\log K}{\varepsilon}\right) \] for every horizon $T$. The numerical constant is not optimized. The algorithm partitions time into blocks of exponentially increasing size, plays a single action throughout each block, and chooses the next action by an exponential mechanism applied to a data-independent random prefix of the previous block. The random prefix converts block regret into a sum, over all prefix lengths, of softmax selection errors. A single entropy-potential argument controls all privacy-dominated large-gap actions at cost $\log K/\varepsilon$.

</details>


### [115] [RUBRIC-ARROW: Alternating Pointwise Rubric Reward Modeling for LLM Post-training in Non-verifiable Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29156)
*Haoxiang Jiang,Zihan Dong,Tianci Liu,Wanying Wang,Ran Xu,Tony Yu,Linjun Zhang,Haoyu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RUBRIC-ARROW框架，通过交替训练评分标准生成器和基于评分标准的评判器，结合概率评分规则和交替GRPO方案，仅使用成对偏好数据实现点式奖励建模，在主观非可验证场景中达到竞争性性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点式奖励建模在主观、非可验证场景中难以进行绝对评分；现有基于评分标准的方法依赖前沿大模型且布尔聚合导致平局问题。

Method: 提出RUBRIC-ARROW交替框架：1) 联合训练评分标准生成器和基于评分标准的评判器；2) RL阶段仅使用成对偏好数据；3) 概率评分规则减少平局；4) 阶段特定偏好奖励；5) 交替GRPO方案训练点式评估器。

Result: 在奖励建模准确率上达到竞争性水平，并为下游策略后训练带来一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: RUBRIC-ARROW有效解决了主观场景中点式奖励建模的难题，通过交替训练和概率评分规则提升了评分质量与下游任务表现。

Abstract: Pointwise reward modeling offers critical signals for LLM post-training, yet struggles with absolute scoring in subjective, non-verifiable settings. Rubric-based methods address this by decomposing evaluation into explicit criteria, but existing approaches typically depend on frontier LLMs and suffer from ties caused by hard Boolean aggregation. We present RUBRIC-ARROW, an alternating framework that jointly trains a rubric generator and a rubric-conditioned judge, with its RL stage using only pairwise preference data. Our method couples a probability-based scoring rule that reduces ties with phase-specific preference-based rewards and an alternating GRPO scheme that together train the pointwise evaluator. Extensive experiments show that RUBRIC-ARROW achieves competitive reward-modeling accuracy and yields consistent gains for downstream policy post-training.

</details>


### [116] [Parallax: Parameterized Local Linear Attention for Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29157)
*Yifei Zuo,Dhruv Pai,Zhichen Zeng,Alec Dewulf,Shuming Hu,Zhaoran Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Parallax，一种可扩展的局部线性注意力机制（Local Linear Attention），通过参数化设计消除数值求解器、引入查询类似投影器探测KV协方差，并设计硬件感知算法提升计算强度，在0.6B和1.7B规模预训练中实现一致困惑度改进和下游任务性能提升，首次展示了注意力机制中架构-优化器协同设计的实证效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有注意力机制（softmax attention）本质是局部常数估计，其偏差-方差权衡在联想记忆任务中存在理论上的次优性。局部线性注意力（LLA）虽能提供更优的偏差-方差权衡，但由于计算复杂度和数值稳定性问题，尚未能在LLM预训练中大规模应用。

Method: Parallax通过参数化设计消除LLA中的数值求解器，学习额外的查询类似投影器来探测KV协方差；将Parallax置于由带宽、探针构造和仿射结构连接的注意力机制家族中；提出硬件感知算法，提升算术强度使注意力进入更计算密集的区域。

Result: 原型解码核在多种批大小和上下文长度下匹配或超越FlashAttention 2/3；0.6B和1.7B规模预训练中，Parallax在参数匹配和计算匹配控制下均实现一致的困惑度改进，并迁移到下游基准任务，展现出Pareto改进。Muon优化器能解锁Parallax的能力。

Conclusion: 本文首次展示了注意力机制中架构-优化器协同设计的实证效果，证明局部线性注意力可扩展至LLM预训练并带来实质性性能提升。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have become the central paradigm in artificial intelligence, yet the core computational primitive of attention has remained structurally unchanged. Local Linear Attention (LLA) is an attention mechanism derived from nonparametric statistics in the test-time regression framework. In contrast to prior research on efficient attention variants, LLA upgrades the local constant estimate in softmax attention to a local linear estimate, yielding provably superior bias-variance tradeoffs for associative memory. However, LLA has not been scaled in LLM pretraining due to computational and numerical stability concerns. We introduce Parallax, a parameterized Local Linear Attention that is scalable for LLMs. Parallax eliminates the numerical solver in LLA and learns an extra query-like projector that probes the KV covariance. We place Parallax within a family of attention mechanisms connected by the bandwidth, the probe construction and the affine structure. We propose a hardware-aware algorithm that increases the arithmetic intensity over FlashAttention, shifting attention into a more compute bound regime. Our prototype decode kernel matches or outperforms FlashAttention 2/3 across diverse batch sizes and context lengths. We pretrain Parallax at 0.6B and 1.7B scales and find consistent perplexity improvements throughout pretraining with gains that transfer to downstream benchmarks. The advantage persists under both parameter-matched and compute-matched controls, demonstrating a Pareto improvement. We perform careful pretraining ablations and identify a novel phenomenon whereby Muon unlocks the capacity of Parallax. To our knowledge, this is the first empirical demonstration of strong architecture-optimizer codesign for attention mechanisms in the architecture research literature.

</details>


### [117] [PROTOCOL: Late Interaction Retrieval for Protein Homolog Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29158)
*Gabrielle Cohn,Rohan Gumaste,Minh Hoang,Vihan Lakshman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ProtoCol使用残基级别的晚期交互（ColBERT风格）代替传统的单向量池化，在SCOPe和Pfam基准测试中优于序列比对、池化PLM和单向量基线，证明残基级比较能有效提升远程同源检索的灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白质同源搜索在" twilight zone"（全局序列相似性弱的区域）灵敏度不足，传统比对方法和基于蛋白质语言模型（PLM）的单向量池化检索会丢失局部基序、结构域和保守残基信息，因此需要更好的表征方法。

Method: ProtoCol将蛋白质编码为残基嵌入集合，采用ColBERT风格的晚期交互（late interaction）机制：独立编码蛋白质（候选表示可预计算），通过MaxSim操作（残基嵌入间的最大相似度求和）对候选进行评分。

Result: 在SCOPe超家族和Pfam clan基准测试上，ProtoCol优于序列组成方法、基于比对的方法、池化PLM方法以及训练的单向量基线方法。

Conclusion: 晚期交互（late interaction）是远程同源搜索中一种有效的检索层设计，残基级别的比较比单向量池化更能捕捉局部保守信息，提升检索灵敏度。

Abstract: Protein homology search underlies function annotation, structure prediction, and evolutionary analysis, but remains challenging in the "twilight zone," where global sequence similarity is weak and classical alignment methods lose sensitivity. Protein language models provide context-aware representations that could improve alignment sensitivity in this regime. However, prior protein embedding-based retrieval pipelines often pool these representations into a single vector, potentially obscuring local motifs, domains, or conserved residues that reveal remote homology. We introduce ProtoCol, a model which represents proteins as sets of residue embeddings and uses ColBERT-style late interaction to test whether residue-level comparison improves homolog retrieval. ProtoCol encodes proteins independently, keeps candidate representations pre-computable, and scores candidates with MaxSim over residue embeddings. On SCOPe superfamily and Pfam clan benchmarks, ProtoCol outperforms sequence-composition, alignment-based, pooled PLM, and trained single-vector baselines, supporting late interaction as an effective retrieval layer for remote homology search.

</details>


### [118] [Evolutionary Refinement of Generative Graph Topologies: A Hybrid WGAN-GA Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29161)
*James Sargant,Seyedeh Ava Razi Razavi,Renata Dividino,Sheridan Houghten*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在GAN生成图框架基础上，使用遗传算法（GA）对生成图的边进行精细化调整，以修正GAN在度分布和谱分布等结构特性上的偏差，实验表明GA优化能持续降低MMD，使生成图更接近真实图结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于GAN的图生成方法虽然在边建模和类条件密度分布匹配上有所改进，但在度分布、谱分布等重要结构属性上仍与真实图存在显著偏差，需要进一步修正这些残留的结构偏差。

Method: 在GAN框架（生成器生成节点特征和连接模式，GNN判别器评估图真实性和类一致性）基础上，引入遗传算法对生成图的边进行演化优化，使合成图在保持多样性和新颖性的同时，更接近真实图的结构分布。

Result: GA精化后的方法在组合MMD指标上持续低于基线GAN模型，生成的图在结构模式上更匹配真实图。

Conclusion: 演化精化是一种有效且灵活的方式，能够修正GAN基图生成器中残留的结构偏差，提升其在真实图合成和数据增强任务中的适用性。

Abstract: Generating realistic graph-structured data is challenging due to discrete connectivity, varying graph sizes, and class-specific structural patterns. Recent Generative Adversarial Networks (GAN)-based graph generation methods improve edge modelling by learning connectivity and matching class-specific density distributions. However these models still exhibit noticeable deviations such as in degree and spectral distribution when compared to real graphs, indicating that important structural properties are not fully preserved. This work aims to reduce these deviations by refining the graphs produced by an existing GAN-based graph generator framework with a Genetic Algorithm (GA). In the GAN framework, the generator produces both node features and connectivity patterns, while a GNN-based critic evaluates graph realism and class consistency to ensure global structural and class alignment. Building on this foundation, we apply a GA to refine the edges of generated graphs. The refinement process guides synthetic graphs toward closer agreement with real data, while preserving diversity and novelty. Experimental results show that the GA refinement consistently lowers combined Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD) compared to the base model, leading to graphs that more closely match real structural patterns. This demonstrates that evolutionary refinement is an effective and flexible way to correct residual structural deviations in GAN-based graph generators, improving their suitability for realistic graph synthesis and data augmentation.

</details>


### [119] [Probabilistic bias adjustment of seasonal forecasts using generative machine learning: A case study of Arctic sea ice predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29172)
*Parsa Gooya,Reinel Sospedra-Alfonso*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于条件变分自编码器（cVAE）的改进概率后处理框架，用于生成大集合、高分辨率、偏差校正的季节性海冰预测。通过用生成器替代高斯参数化解码器、采用连续排序概率评分（CRPS）替代均方误差作为目标函数，并使用更高分辨率的目标数据集，该方法在保持校准性的同时，显著提升了预测的清晰度、锐度和谱能量，克服了标准cVAE的模糊性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 季节性气候预测中，大集合和高分辨率预报虽能更好地捕捉不确定性和精细过程，但计算成本高昂；同时预报集合存在漂移、系统偏差和时空误差，需要细致的后处理和校准。现有基于cVAE的后处理方法存在预测模糊、细尺度能量丢失的问题，亟需改进。

Method: 1) 用生成器（generator）替代标准cVAE中的高斯参数化解码器；2) 在目标函数中使用连续排序概率评分（CRPS）替代均方误差（MSE）；3) 使用比原始预报更高分辨率的目标数据集进行训练。

Result: 改进后的预测在校准性、与观测分布的一致性、误差大小方面均优于基准预测；同时相比标准cVAE，显著提升了原始预报的分辨率、锐度和谱能量（即细尺度能量）。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了标准cVAE后处理中预测模糊、丢失细尺度能量的问题，能够生成校准良好、高分辨率的大集合季节性海冰预测，对气候风险评估和规划具有重要价值。

Abstract: Seasonal climate predictions support planning and risk management by offering early information of the most likely-to-occur climate conditions in the coming months, and associated uncertainties. Ensemble forecasts enable this by simulating many plausible outcomes, allowing predictions to be expressed as usable probabilities. Large ensembles and high-resolution forecasts strengthen this guidance by better sampling uncertainty and capturing finer-scale processes but come with significant computational cost. Moreover, forecast ensembles drift and exhibit systematic biases and spatio-temporal errors that grow with lead time, requiring careful post-processing and calibration. A probabilistic post-processing framework based on conditional Variational Autoencoders (cVAEs) was developed at the Canadian Center for Climate Modeling and Analysis to generate large ensembles of bias adjusted seasonal predictions of Arctic sea ice. The generative model was designed to learn the observational distribution conditioned on the biased model prediction. This enables generation of arbitrarily large ensembles of well-calibrated, bias corrected forecasts with improved skill. Here, we extend this framework to address the loss of fine-scale energy and the characteristic blurriness in predictions, a known limitation of standard cVAEs. Specifically, we employ a generator in place of the Gaussian parametrized decoder in the cVAE and use Continuous Ranked Probability Score in the objective function instead of the Mean Square Error. We further use a higher resolution target dataset compared to the raw forecast. We show that the adjusted forecasts are better calibrated, more consistent with the observational distribution, and exhibit smaller errors than benchmark predictions, while also enhancing the resolution of the raw forecasts and improving sharpness and spectral power relative to the standard cVAE.

</details>


### [120] [TIMEGATE: Sustainable Time-Boxed Promotion Gates for Continual ML Adaptation Under Resource Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29183)
*Abhijit Chakrabroty,Suddhasvatta Das,Kevin A. Gary,Yash Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TIMEGATE是一个策略管理层，通过预算时间、标注、训练和评估来管理持续适应中的资源分配。实验表明：标注比训练效率高2.3倍；在LLaMA-3.1-8B+QLoRA上准确率从0.80提升至0.96；在100轮模拟中实现66%的评估计算节省且无静默误提升；10%切片评估节省89%的壁钟时间和能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习系统需要持续适应，但每次重训练周期消耗大量计算、标注和能源资源，需要更高效的资源管理策略。

Method: 提出TIMEGATE策略管理层，通过预算时间、标注、训练和评估来管理适应过程，并发射度量可用性信号M用于部分评估与完全评估的决策。

Result: 标注比训练效率高2.3倍；在LLaMA-3.1-8B+QLoRA上从0.80提升至0.96（M=1在35/36次运行）；28单元敏感性分析显示M在严格阈值下降至0.81；100轮模拟节省66%评估计算且无静默误提升；10%切片评估节省89%壁钟时间和能耗。

Conclusion: TIMEGATE能有效减少持续学习中的计算、标注和能源开销，同时保证模型性能不受影响，具有良好的可迁移性和实用性。

Abstract: As machine learning(ML) systems evolve to continual adaptation, each re-training cycle uses compute, annotation, and energy. We introduce TIMEGATE, a policy layer managing adaptation by budgeting time, labeling, training, and evaluation. TIMEGATE emits a metric-availability signal M for partial vs. full-evaluation decisions. We validate: (i) labeling outperforms training by 2.3x on Adult tabular; (ii) it transfers to LLaMA-3.1-8B + QLoRA on SST-2 (accuracy 0.80 to 0.96; M =1 in 35/36 runs); (iii) M is informative, 28-cell sensitivity shows M drops to 0.81 at tight thresholds; (iv) 100-cycle simulation achieves 66% evaluation-compute savings with no silent mis-promotions; (v) 10%-slice evaluation on LLaMA uses 89% less wall-clock and energy on a single H200 (ratios agree to 0.2%).

</details>


### [121] [Influence-Guided Symbolic Regression: Scientific Discovery via LLM-Driven Equation Search with Granular Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29184)
*Evgeny S. Saveliev,Samuel Holt,Nabeel Seedat,David L. Bentley,Jim Weatherall,Mihaela van der Schaar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为IGSR（影响力引导的符号回归）方法，将LLM与蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）结合，通过每个项对泛化准确率的边际贡献（影响力分数）来引导模型结构的剪枝与优化，在多个基准上表现优异，并在一项真实生物学数据中发现了DNA甲基化与RNA聚合酶II暂停之间的新关系，经湿实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM应用于符号回归的方法存在两个主要问题：一是使用全局标量指标（如MSE）作为反馈信号过于粗糙，无法识别方程中哪些部分贡献了性能、哪些导致了误差；二是搜索策略效率低下。作者希望提出一种细粒度的、可解释的反馈机制来指导符号回归过程。

Method: IGSR将方程发现建模为迭代两步过程：1）LLM生成候选基函数ψ_j(x)用于线性模型；2）计算每个项的影响力分数Δ_j（量化该项对泛化准确率的边际贡献），基于此进行剪枝以精炼模型结构。将该机制集成到MCTS中，在组合搜索空间中平衡探索新函数形式与利用高影响力成分。

Result: 在LLM-SRBench、药理学PKPD模型、流行病学模拟及真实基因组数据等多类基准上验证了有效性。关键发现：在真实高维生物学数据案例中，IGSR发现了DNA甲基化与RNA聚合酶II暂停之间的新关系，该假设随后通过湿实验得到验证。

Conclusion: IGSR通过引入影响力导向的细粒度反馈机制，有效提升了LLM在符号回归中的搜索效率与发现能力，不仅提高了模型性能，还具备真正的科学发现潜力，为AI驱动的科学发现提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) offer a promising avenue for scientific discovery, yet their application to symbolic regression is often constrained by inefficient search strategies and coarse feedback signals. Current methods typically guide LLMs using scalar metrics (e.g., global Mean Squared Error), which fail to identify which components of a proposed equation are driving performance or causing error. We introduce \textit{Influence-Guided Symbolic Regression} (IGSR), a method that frames equation discovery as an iterative two-step process combining diverse term generation with rigorous selection: an LLM generates candidate basis functions $ψ_j(\mathbf{x})$ for a linear model, which are then evaluated using granular influence scores $Δ_j$. These scores quantify each term's marginal contribution to generalization accuracy, enabling an influence-guided pruning process that systematically refines the model structure. Integrating this mechanism into a Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) enables navigating the combinatorial search space while balancing exploration of novel functional forms with exploitation of high-influence components. We demonstrate IGSR's effectiveness on a diverse suite of benchmarks, including LLM-SRBench, pharmacological PKPD models, an epidemiological simulation, and real-world genomic data. Notably, we validate the framework's capacity for genuine discovery in a case study using a high-dimensional biological dataset, in which IGSR identified a novel relationship between DNA methylation and RNA Polymerase II pausing; a hypothesis that was subsequently supported via wet-lab experimentation.

</details>


### [122] [When RL Suppresses Its Own Vocabulary: Recovering Reasoning Diversity in Puzzle-to-Math Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29190)
*Mayug Maniparambil,Arjun Karuvally,Terrence Sejnowski,Fergal Reid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了仅使用约束满足谜题（不含数学题）对LLM进行后训练（SFT+RL）如何提升数学推理能力，并提出了一个推理基元级分析框架。作者发现：SFT阶段诱导出推理基元词汇表，GSPO阶段组合基元形成更长推理链但抑制探索性基元，引入新颖性奖励可恢复探索性基元。最终方案将OLMo3-7B在困难数学上的pass@32从16.0%提升至36.0%，且训练数据中不含任何数学题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）能提升LLM推理能力，但其跨领域迁移的条件和内在机制尚未被充分探索。本文希望通过精细的基元级分析揭示推理能力从谜题领域迁移到数学领域的原理。

Method: 提出推理基元级分析框架（9类跨度分类器+动机提取），将思维链分解为基元并追踪其演化；引入新颖性奖励（以参考模型下的困惑度为信号）来奖励多样化的正确轨迹，以缓解标准RL对探索性基元（hypothesize, backtrack）的抑制。

Result: SFT阶段建立推理基元词汇表，+7pp pass@32；GSPO阶段组合基元形成验证链，+6pp；新颖性奖励恢复探索性基元，再+7pp。最终方案不使用任何数学训练数据，将困难数学pass@32从16.0%提升至36.0%。

Conclusion: 推理基元可跨领域迁移，谜题训练足以激发数学推理能力；标准RL可能抑制有用的探索性推理行为，而新颖性奖励能有效恢复这些行为并进一步提升性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning using verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves LLM reasoning, but the conditions under which it transfers across domains -- and why it does so -- remain under-explored. We study cross-domain transfer in a 7B model whose SFT and RL post-training stages use only constraint-satisfaction puzzles, with no mathematics problems in the post-training data. To analyze how transfer emerges, we introduce a reasoning primitive-level framework that combines a 9-class span classifier with motif extraction, allowing us to segment chain-of-thought traces into primitive motifs and track their evolution across training stages and domains. We find that puzzle SFT induces a reasoning-primitive vocabulary, yielding a $+7$pp \texttt{pass@32} gain on OlymMATH-Hard. Vanilla GSPO then composes these primitives into longer compute-verify chains, adding a further $+6$pp. However, this RL stage also suppresses exploratory primitives such as \textit{hypothesize} and \textit{backtrack}. To address this, we introduce a novelty bonus that rewards diverse correct rollouts, using perplexity under the reference model as a signal. This restores recovery primitives during RL and adds a further $+7$pp \texttt{pass@32} relative to vanilla GSPO. Finally, the end-to-end recipe raises the hard-math capability ceiling from $16.0\%$ at the OLMo3-7B-Instruct-SFT base to $36.0\%$, without adding any mathematics problems during the SFT or RL stages.

</details>


### [123] [Auditing Training Data in Generative Music Models via Black-Box Membership Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29202)
*Yi Chen Liu,Jiawei Yu,Kexin Cao,Syed Irfan Ali Meerza,Trishika Movva,Jian Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对文本到音乐生成模型的黑盒成员推断方法，通过候选音频与模型条件生成输出之间的语义和结构对齐程度来判断该音频是否属于训练集，在多个SOTA模型上达到最高98.6%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前文本到音乐生成模型通常在大规模数据集上训练但缺乏数据来源、同意和训练透明度的披露，没有实用机制验证特定音频样本是否被用于训练，因此需要一种黑盒成员推断方法来审计训练数据。

Method: 核心思路是训练成员会使得候选样本与模型基于其描述生成的结果之间存在更强的语义和结构对齐。具体方法：通过影子模型构建（候选音频及其描述条件生成的）配对样本，训练一个音乐审计器来学习区分成员与非成员的特征，该审计器完全在黑盒设置下运行，无需访问模型参数或训练元数据。

Result: 在多个最先进的音乐生成模型上，该方法达到最高98.6%的准确率，假阳性率低至1.9%，假阴性率低至1.0%。

Conclusion: 证明了在现实部署场景下对训练数据进行可靠审计是可行的，为生成式音乐模型的数据溯源和合规提供了有效手段。

Abstract: Recent advances in text-to-music generation enable high-fidelity synthesis of structured musical audio, raising growing concerns about data provenance, consent, and training transparency. These models are typically trained on large-scale corpora with little disclosure, leaving no practical mechanism to verify whether a particular audio sample was included in training. In this paper, we investigate black-box membership inference for generative music models, aiming to determine whether a candidate music sample was used during training, given only query access to the deployed system. Our key insight is that training membership induces systematically stronger semantic and structural alignment between a candidate sample and the model's generation conditioned on its caption. We query the target model with the associated caption and measure the relationship between the candidate audio and the generated output in a learned feature space. To capture features that separate members from non-members, we construct paired examples consisting of each track and its caption-conditioned generation from shadow models, and train a music auditor to classify membership. The auditor captures alignment patterns characteristic of training membership and generalizes to unseen target models in a fully black-box setting without access to model parameters or training metadata. Across multiple state-of-the-art music generators, our method achieves up to 98.6% accuracy, with false-positive and false-negative rates as low as 1.9% and 1.0%, demonstrating that reliable training-data auditing is feasible in realistic deployment scenarios.

</details>


### [124] [Inferring the Size of Large Language Models From Popular Text Memorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29223)
*Ivica Nikolic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种黑盒方法，仅通过分析模型对公开文本的预测准确性，就能推断大语言模型的参数规模下限，无需访问模型内部信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流大语言模型的参数规模常被开发者隐瞒，而模型大小是理解其能力和成本的关键参考指标，亟需一种无需内部信息即可推断模型规模的可靠方法。

Method: 基于流行文本广泛存在于预训练语料中的观察，通过不同长度文本片段的下一词预测准确性构建每个模型的准确性剖面向量，进而提出两种互补推断方法：成对统计检验（比较两个模型大小）和基于PCA的缩放律估计器（将聚合信号映射为参数数值）。

Result: 在开放权重模型上验证了两种方法的准确性和可靠性；应用于闭源模型时，恢复了内部产品层级结构，揭示了不同开发者在行业扩展策略上的明显差异（部分采用大规模代际参数增长，部分受严格参数上限约束）。

Conclusion: 即使在严格的API限制下，也能系统性地探测隐藏的设计选择，表明模型参数规模可通过文本输出进行有效推断。

Abstract: The parameter counts of the most widely used large language models (LLMs) are often withheld by their developers, leaving model size -- a primary reference point for interpreting capabilities and costs -- largely undisclosed. We propose a black-box method to infer conservative lower bounds on LLM size from generated text outputs alone, requiring nothing beyond the ability to submit text fragments and observe next-token predictions. Our approach is grounded in a key observation: popular, widely-circulated texts -- such as classical literature, religious texts, and foundational documents -- are present in virtually every large-scale pretraining corpus, and how accurately a model predicts the next word across text fragments of varying length is a reliable signal of how much it has memorized them, which in turn is fundamentally limited by its total parameter count. We aggregate this memorization signal across a diverse corpus of texts and fragment lengths into a single accuracy profile vector per model, and build two complementary inference methods on top of it: a pairwise statistical test that determines which of two models is larger, and a scaling-law estimator that extracts a one-dimensional latent index from these vectors via Principal Component Analysis (PCA) to map the aggregated signal to a parameter count. Validated on a broad set of open-weight models, both methods produce accurate and reliable lower bounds. When applied to popular closed-weight models, our framework recovers internal product hierarchies and reveals a clear divergence in industry scaling strategies: while some developers yield significantly higher bounds indicative of large generational parameter growth, others operate under strict parameter ceilings, demonstrating that hidden design choices can be systematically probed even under strict API limitations.

</details>


### [125] [BlockBatch: Multi-Scale Consensus Decoding for Efficient Diffusion Language Model Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29233)
*Xiaoyou Wu,Cheng-Jhih Shih,Binfei Ji,Yong Liu,Yingyan,Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BlockBatch，一种无需训练的多分支并行推理框架，通过利用不同块大小的互补性来加速扩散语言模型。该方法在单个批次内并行执行多个块大小分支，并通过置信度门控令牌合并、领导者同步和周期性全序列刷新来协调分支，在保持精度的同时平均减少26.6%的去噪NFE，实现1.33倍的端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型在块级推理时面临粒度权衡：小块保留局部条件但需要多步去噪，大块增加并行性但可能过早承诺并累积缓存误差。现有加速方法对每个请求使用单一固定块大小，忽略了不同块大小之间的互补性。

Method: 提出BlockBatch训练无关在线推理框架：(1) 并行执行多个不同块大小的分支在同一批前向传播中；(2) 置信度门控令牌合并；(3) 基于领导者的同步机制；(4) 周期性全序列刷新以将局部块更新重新锚定到全局一致的KV状态。

Result: 在3种代表性dLLMs和4个数据集上，BlockBatch平均减少26.6%的去噪NFE，相对Fast-dLLM实现1.33倍端到端加速，同时保持准确率不变。

Conclusion: 块大小多样性是一个实用且此前未被充分探索的用于分支并行dLLM推理的优化轴。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (dLLMs) generate text by iteratively denoising multiple token positions in parallel, offering an attractive alternative to strictly autoregressive decoding. In practice, however, block-wise dLLM inference exposes a difficult granularity trade-off: small blocks preserve local conditioning but require many denoising steps, whereas large blocks expose more parallelism but can make premature commitments and accumulate cache error. Existing acceleration methods typically choose a single block size per request, leaving the complementarity among block sizes unused. We show that block size itself is a useful branching dimension. Different block sizes induce related but non-identical KV-cache trajectories: branches often share an initial prefix, bifurcate at semantically decisive positions, and later agree on syntactically lightweight tokens. Motivated by this structure, we propose BlockBatch, a training-free online inference framework that executes multiple block-size branches for the same request inside a batched forward pass. BlockBatch coordinates these branches through confidence-gated token merging, leader-based synchronization, and periodic full-sequence refreshes that re-anchor local block updates to a globally consistent KV state. Across 3 representative dLLMs and 4 datasets, BlockBatch reduces denoising NFEs by 26.6\% on average and achieves a 1.33$\times$ average end-to-end speedup over Fast-dLLM while preserving accuracy. These results identify block-size diversity as a practical and previously underexplored axis for branch-parallel dLLM inference.

</details>


### [126] [KLAS: Using Similarity to Stitch Neural Networks for Improved Accuracy-Efficiency Tradeoffs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29259)
*Debopam Sanyal,Anantharaman Iyer,Alind Khare,Trisha Jain,Akshay Jajoo,Myungjin Lee,Clayton Kerce,Alexey Tumanov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出KLAS框架，利用KL散度自动选择最优的模型拼接配置，在给定计算预算下实现精度-效率的更好权衡，相比基线方法在ImageNet-1K上提升最高1.21%的Top-1精度或减少1.33倍FLOPs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型拼接方法依赖启发式规则选择拼接配置，导致精度-效率权衡次优且缺乏泛化性。作者认为需要显式捕获并利用预训练模型之间的相似性来构建更优的拼接方案。

Method: 提出KLAS框架，通过计算预训练模型中间表示的KL散度来量化模型间的相似性，从O(k²n²)种可能的二元拼接中自动选择最优拼接配置，实现跨模型族的自动化泛化拼接选择。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K上，KLAS在相同计算成本下Top-1精度提升最高1.21%，或在保持精度前提下FLOPs减少1.33倍，且微调成本与基线相同。

Conclusion: KLAS通过KL散度驱动的自动拼接选择，有效提升了拼接模型的精度-效率帕累托前沿，为灵活模型部署提供了更优的插值方案。

Abstract: Given the wide range of deployment targets, flexible model selection is essential for optimizing performance within a given compute budget. Recent work demonstrates that stitching pretrained models within a model family enables cost-effective interpolation of the accuracy-efficiency tradeoff space. Stitching transforms intermediate activations from one pretrained model into another, producing a new interpolated stitched network. Such networks provide a pool of deployment options along the accuracy-efficiency spectrum. However, existing stitching approaches often yield suboptimal tradeoffs and lack generalizability, as they primarily rely on heuristics to select stitch configurations. We argue that constructing improved accuracy-efficiency tradeoffs requires explicitly capturing and leveraging the similarity between pretrained models being stitched. To this end, we introduce KLAS, a novel stitch selection framework that automates and generalizes stitch selection across model families by leveraging KL divergence between intermediate representations. KLAS identifies the most promising binary stitches from the $O(k^2n^2)$ possibilities for $k$ pretrained models of depth $n$. Through comprehensive experiments, we demonstrate that KLAS improves the accuracy-efficiency curve of stitched models at the same finetuning cost as baselines. KLAS achieves up to $1.21\%$ higher ImageNet-1K top-1 accuracy at the same computational cost, or maintains accuracy with a $1.33\times$ reduction in FLOPs.

</details>


### [127] [Robust Frequency-Calibrated Virtual EEG Channel Generation from Four Frontal Electrodes for Wearable EEG Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29263)
*Minghao Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FAVC-Net利用四个额叶电极（Fp1, Fp2, F7, F8）生成13个未测量的EEG通道，通过频域校准和双域增强策略，在保持波形保真度的同时显著降低频谱距离（30.09%）和PSD KL散度（37.98%），并抗穿戴式噪声干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低通道可穿戴EEG（仅4个额叶电极）对长时间监测有吸引力，但空间覆盖稀疏且存在偏置，无法获取后部和顶叶区域的脑电活动信息。

Method: 提出FAVC-Net，包含共享多尺度源编码、源状态嵌入、目标条件符号源块混合、GATv2注意力精化、注意力一致跳跃融合及弱Welch功率谱密度校准，联合优化波形幅度、频谱分配、通道频率纹理和抗损鲁棒性。

Result: 在PRED+CT数据集上，FAVC-Net达到最佳波形-频谱联合性能；时域提升温和，但对数谱距离降低30.09%，PSD KL散度降低37.98%；在模拟可穿戴扰动下保持频谱保真度并抵抗频谱坍塌。

Conclusion: 虚拟EEG通道生成应被视为双域增强问题；生成的后部和顶叶通道应解释为源自稀疏额叶测量的频率校准表示，而非独立物理记录。

Abstract: Low-channel wearable electroencephalography (EEG) is attractive for long-term monitoring, but four frontal electrodes provide only a sparse and spatially biased view of distributed scalp activity. We present FAVC-Net, a compact frequency-calibrated virtual-channel network that generates 13 unmeasured EEG channels from Fp1, Fp2, F7, and F8. The model combines shared multi-scale source encoding, source-state embeddings, target-conditioned signed source-block mixing, GATv2-based attention refinement, attention-consistent skip fusion, and weak Welch power spectral density calibration. Rather than treating sparse-to-dense EEG generation as a purely waveform-matching task, the framework jointly emphasizes amplitude fidelity, spectral allocation, channel-frequency texture, and robustness to corrupted wearable inputs. On the PRED+CT dataset, FAVC-Net achieved the best joint waveform-spectral operating point among neural and interpolation baselines. Its time-domain gains were modest, whereas log-spectral distance and PSD KL divergence were reduced by 30.09% and 37.98% relative to the strongest non-FAVC comparator. Under wearable-like source perturbations, the model preserved spectral fidelity and resisted spectral collapse. These results support virtual EEG channel generation as a dual-domain augmentation problem, while emphasizing that generated posterior and parietal channels should be interpreted as frequency-calibrated representations derived from sparse frontal measurements rather than as independent physical recordings.

</details>


### [128] [LoopFM: Learning frOm HistOrical RePresentations of Foundation Model for Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29280)
*Shali Jiang,Hua Zheng,Boyang Liu,Laming Chen,Kenny Lov,Chuanqi Xu,Lisang Ding,Qinghai Zhou,Can Cui,Xiaolong Liu,Xiaoyi Liu,Yasmine Badr,Xin Xu,Jiyan Yang,Ellie Dingqiao Wen,Gerard Jonathan Mugisha Akkerhuis,Chenxiao Guan,Rong Jin,Ruichao Qiu,Xian Chen,Shifu Xu,Zhehui Zhou,Ping Chen,Rui Yang,Haicheng Chen,Xiangge Meng,Song Zhou,Dharak Kharod,Shuyu Xu,Qiang Jin,Qiao Yang,Wankun Zhu,Qin Huang,Yuzhen Huang,Darren Liu,Parish Aggarwal,Hui Zhou,Erzhuo Wang,Shuo Chang,Xiaorui Gan,Wenlin Chen,Santanu Kolay,Huayu Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LoopFM通过将大模型中间层表示结构化为下游小模型的特征输入，构建高带宽知识传输通道，显著提升知识迁移效率，在工业级系统中相比KD方法实现知识迁移率翻倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统知识蒸馏仅传输单个标量预测，导致知识迁移率随模型规模增大而衰减，无法充分利用大模型习得的丰富中间表征。

Method: 将大模型的中间层嵌入结构化为下游任务的特征输入（如用户历史序列），在不依赖实时大模型推理和架构耦合的前提下，构建高带宽知识传输通道。提供了包含增益分解和迁移率分析的理论框架。

Result: 在TaobaoAd上实现6%+的AUC提升；在工业级系统（百亿样本、万亿参数）中，在KD基础上将知识迁移率约翻倍，Y1H1转化率提升0.5%，Y1H2两个独立上线分别带来1.03%和1.22%的转化率提升。

Conclusion: LoopFM有效突破了传统KD的标量瓶颈，与KD具有互补性，能显著提升大模型向小模型的知识迁移效率。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation (KD) transfers a single scalar prediction from a large foundation model (FM) to compact vertical models (VMs), suffering from diminishing transfer ratio -- the fraction of FM improvement captured by the VM -- as a single scalar cannot convey the rich intermediate knowledge that larger FMs learn. To address this bottleneck, we propose LoopFM (Learning frOm HistOrical ReP*resentations of FM), a framework that opens a high-bandwidth transfer channel by structuring FM intermediate embeddings as input features (e.g., user history sequence) for downstream VMs, without requiring real-time FM inference at serving and architectural coupling between FM and VM. We provide a theoretical framework for LoopFM with a gain decomposition and transfer-ratio analysis. On three public benchmarks, LoopFM demonstrates strong AUC improvements (e.g., 6\%+ on TaobaoAd) and complementary knowledge transfer capability with KD. On industrial-scale systems (billions of examples, trillion-parameter FMs), LoopFM approximately doubles the knowledge transfer ratio on top of KD, delivering a +0.5\% conversion improvement in Y1H1, and a +1.03\% and +1.22\% conversion improvement from two individual launches respectively in Y1H2.

</details>


### [129] [Do Physics Foundation Models Learn Generalizable Physics? A Bias-Aware Benchmark Across Physical Regimes and Distribution Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29283)
*Mengdi Chu,Yang Liu,Ayan Biswas,Han-Wei Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 当前物理基础模型声称具备通用时空预测能力，但实验表明它们实际是条件性通用而非真正通用：其泛化能力高度依赖物理场景、时间尺度、初始条件、预训练、模型规模和架构，且扩大数据和模型规模无法可靠消除这些能力偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理基础模型评估通常只在固定训练分布下报告单一平均分数，无法判断模型是否真正学习了可泛化的物理动力学，还是在特定设置下表现良好。需要系统性地评估模型在不同物理动力学、训练数据混合和测试场景下的泛化能力。

Method: 构建包含8种物理动力学、3种训练数据混合和25种测试场景（涵盖分布内、分布偏移和分布外）的基准测试。评估5种物理基础模型架构，每种架构4种变体（从零训练和3种预训练规模），共获得60,000个测量结果。

Result: 当前物理基础模型表现为条件性通用而非真正通用；其泛化能力取决于物理场景、时间尺度、初始条件、预训练、模型规模和架构；改进训练数据分布只能部分缓解此限制；预训练和规模缩放也无法可靠消除能力偏差。

Conclusion: 改进物理基础模型需要超越简单缩放模型或扩展数据，转向学习能更好捕获跨场景、时间尺度和分布偏移的可迁移物理知识的机制。

Abstract: Recent physics foundation models claim general spatiotemporal forecasting ability, yet their evaluations often collapse performance into a single average score under a fixed training distribution. This makes it difficult to determine whether a model has learned generalizable physical dynamics or only performs well under particular settings. We construct a benchmark with 8 physical dynamics, 3 training-data mixtures, and 25 test regimes induced by dynamic-scale and initial-condition complexity shifts, covering in-distribution, distribution-shift, and out-of-distribution settings. We evaluate five physics foundation model architectures and four model variants per architecture (scratch and three pretrained sizes), resulting in 60,000 measurements. Our results show that current physics foundation models behave as conditional rather than universal generalists: their generality depends on the physical regime, temporal scale, initial-condition setting, pretraining, model size, and architecture. Improving the training data distribution only partially mitigates this limitation. Pretraining and scaling are also unable to reliably remove their ability biases. We argue that improving physics foundation models requires moving beyond scaling models or expanding data, toward learning mechanisms that better capture transferable physical knowledge across regimes, temporal scales, and distribution shifts.

</details>


### [130] [NeuroEdge: Real-Time Hand Gesture Recognition with High-Density EMG Using Deep Learning at the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29326)
*Peter Chudinov,Zhenyu Lin,Jay Motamarry,Srihita Panati,Xiaorong Zhang,Zhuwei Qin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NeuroEdge系统，在资源受限的微控制器上实现基于高密度肌电信号(HD-EMG)的实时手势识别，通过ESP32与Sony Spresense双微控制器架构、无线流传输和轻量级1D-CNN推理引擎，在192通道HD-EMG上达到90%准确率和83ms平均延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管深度学习在HD-EMG手势识别中表现出高精度，但其在嵌入式硬件上的部署仍面临计算和内存限制的巨大挑战，现有方案难以在资源受限的微控制器上实现实时推理。

Method: 设计双模块架构：HD-EMG StreamBridge（Quattrocento放大器→ESP32无线数据流传输）和EdgeDL Inference Engine（Sony Spresense上运行的轻量级1D-CNN）；通过DMA数据传输和SPI突发通信实现流水线同步，确保低延迟。

Result: 在192通道前臂HD-EMG记录的七种手势上实现90%实时分类准确率，总平均延迟83ms。

Conclusion: 证明了在微控制器级边缘设备上部署复杂HD-EMG手势识别的可行性，弥合了高分辨率生物信号采集与深度学习嵌入式推理之间的差距，为下一代神经-机器接口铺平了道路。

Abstract: High-density electromyography (HD-EMG) has emerged as a powerful modality for decoding fine-grained neuromuscular activity, enabling real-time neural-machine interfaces (NMIs) for applications such as prosthetic control, rehabilitation, and augmented interaction. While deep learning approaches such as convolutional neural networks (CNNs)have demonstrated high classification accuracy for EMG-based gesture recognition, their deployment on embedded hardware remains a major challenge due to computational and memory constraints. This paper presents NeuroEdge, a real-time HD EMG-based NMI system that performs gesture recognition entirely on resource-constrained microcontrollers. The system features two custom-designed modules: the HD-EMG StreamBridge, a wireless communication interface that streams raw HD-EMG data from a Quattrocento amplifier to an ESP32 microcontroller; and the EdgeDL Inference Engine, a lightweight deep learning framework executing on a Sony Spresense microcontroller. A compact 1-dimensional CNN optimized for embedded inference processes, sliding windows of EMG data in real time. Data streaming and inference are pipelined and synchronized through an architecture that utilizes Direct Memory Access (DMA) for data transfer and Serial Peripheral Interface (SPI) burst communication between the ESP32 and Spresense, ensuring low-latency performance. Experimental results show that NeuroEdge achieves a real-time classification accuracy of 90% across seven hand gestures, with a total average latency of 83 ms using 192 channels of HD-EMG recorded from the forearm. Our system demonstrates the feasibility of deploying complex HD-EMG-based gesture recognition on microcontroller-based edge devices, bridging the gap between high-resolution biosignal acquisition and deep learning-based embedded inference for next-generation NMIs.

</details>


### [131] [Attention as In-Context Empirical Bayes: A Two-Stage View via Particle Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29351)
*Matthew Smart,Soumya Ganguly,Nilava Metya,Alexandre V. Morozov,Anirvan M. Sengupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明最小化仅含注意力机制的Transformer在全部token被破坏的情况下，具有两阶段经验贝叶斯解释：注意力层计算核加权后验均值，深度通过粒子动力学细化分布（阶段1），长程跳跃连接携带噪声输入作为后验推理的查询（阶段2），揭示了深度和注意力残差的区别性统计角色。研究显示，固定的核带宽和有限的积分范围足以产生有效去噪，无需显式噪声调度，并建立了后验均值恢复的理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在理解Transformer中注意力机制在上下文学习中的统计角色，特别是在所有token被破坏的极端情况下，注意力层是否以及如何实现贝叶斯推理。

Method: 采用最小化仅注意力Transformer架构，在全部token被破坏的设定下，从经验贝叶斯视角分析注意力计算的统计含义；通过粒子动力学解释深度层的作用，并建立核带宽与深度之间的理论关系。

Result: 注意力单层计算等价于核加权后验均值；深度层执行粒子动力学细化（阶段1），跳跃连接将噪声输入作为后验推理查询（阶段2）；固定的核带宽和有限积分范围即可实现有效去噪；对一类良好先验建立了后验均值恢复的理论保证。

Conclusion: 注意力机制可解释为基于样本的后验估计实现上下文推理，无需显式密度建模；深度与注意力残差具有不同的统计功能，且有效去噪可以在无显式噪声调度的情况下自发涌现。

Abstract: We study minimal attention-only transformers under all-token corruption and show they admit a two-stage empirical Bayes interpretation. A single attention step computes a kernel-weighted posterior mean with respect to the empirical distribution defined by the context. Depth refines this distribution through particle dynamics (Stage 1), while a long-range skip-connection carries the noisy input as a query for posterior inference (Stage 2), revealing distinct statistical roles for depth and attention residuals. The framework isolates a minimal setting in which the context itself induces a depth-dependent energy landscape governing in-context inference. We show that effective denoising can emerge without an explicit noise schedule: a fixed kernel bandwidth and finite integration horizon suffice, yielding a principled depth-noise relationship. We further establish a posterior-mean recovery guarantee for a class of well-behaved priors, where the empirical estimator converges to the Bayes-optimal predictor under asymptotic conditions. Connecting these dynamics to reverse-diffusion limits, our results provide a statistical interpretation of attention as in-context inference via sample-based posterior estimation, without explicit density modeling.

</details>


### [132] [Neural-Behavioral Representation of Natural Whole-body Movement in Monkeys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29355)
*Jieshi He,Puzhe Li,Yanan Sui,Mu-ming Poo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合大规模硬膜外皮层信号与多视角运动捕捉的自由运动猴子神经-行为记录与建模框架，通过自回归编码器-解码器模型学习紧凑行为先验，实现了无需显式物理约束的全身运动解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究受限于运动多样性不足和全身运动神经表征难以获取，主要聚焦于受限任务和有限肢体运动，缺乏对灵长类自然全身行为的神经解码方法。

Method: 1) 构建定制化数据采集平台，同步记录自由运动猴子分布式感觉运动区的大规模硬膜外皮层信号与多视角运动捕捉数据；2) 使用自回归编码器-解码器模型学习紧凑的全身运动行为先验；3) 以神经信号为条件，解码准确且自然的全身运动。

Result: 模型无需显式物理约束即可从神经信号中解码出准确且逼真的全身运动。

Conclusion: 该工作为利用大规模颅内神经活动解码灵长类自然全身运动提供了新的概念验证方法。

Abstract: Understanding how cortical activity represents natural whole-body behaviors in primates remains challenging. Limited by the diversity of movements and inaccessibility of large-scale neural representation of whole-body kinematics, previous motor decoding studies focused on constrained tasks and limited limb movements. Here, we present a neural-behavioral recording and modeling framework for freely moving monkeys, combining large-scale epidural cortical signals from distributed sensory- and motor-related areas with synchronized multi-view motion capture through a custom-made data collection platform. We reconstructed whole-body monkey kinematics and learned a compact behavior prior using an autoregressive encoder-decoder model. Conditioned on neural signals, the model decoded accurate and realistic whole-body movement without explicit physical constraints. Our results provide a novel proof-of-concept approach for decoding natural whole-body movements in primates using large-scale intracranial neural activity.

</details>


### [133] [Solving Integer Linear Programming with Parallel Tempering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29366)
*Kyuil Sim,Sanghyeok Choi,Jinkyoo Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种无求解器、基于采样的整数线性规划优化框架，通过局部平衡提议和并行回火（含标准温度回火和罚函数回火）直接探索离散可行域，无需训练或外部求解器，在多个基准上表现优于SCIP，与Gurobi竞争力相当，且对分布偏移具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的ILP方法存在泛化性差（对分布外实例）和依赖外部求解器的固有问题，亟需一种不依赖训练和外部求解器的替代方案。

Method: 提出无求解器的采样优化框架：利用ILP线性结构设计局部平衡提议构造转移核（避免梯度近似），结合并行回火（标准温度回火+罚函数回火）克服能量景观的多模态性，其中罚函数回火在保持可行解目标景观的同时调节约束屏障。

Result: 在四个基准上持续优于SCIP；在200秒预算内，两项任务匹配或超越Gurobi；相比学习方法对分布偏移更具鲁棒性；在MIPLIB 2017实例上无需问题特定调参即可与经典求解器保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 所提出的无训练、无外部求解器的采样框架在ILP优化中展现出竞争性能、强鲁棒性和良好泛化能力，为线性整数规划提供了一种新的求解范式。

Abstract: Integer Linear Programming (ILP) serves as a versatile framework for modeling a wide range of combinatorial optimization problems, typically addressed by sophisticated exact solvers or heuristics. While learning-based approaches have recently shown their effectiveness, they suffer from poor generalization to out-of-distribution instances and inherent dependence on external solvers. In this work, we propose a solver-free, sampling-based optimization framework for ILP that directly explores discrete feasible regions without training or external solvers. Exploiting the linear structure of ILP, we employ a Locally-Balanced Proposal to construct a transition kernel, thereby avoiding the gradient approximation. To overcome the highly multimodal nature of ILP energy landscapes, we integrate Parallel Tempering. In addition to standard temperature tempering, we introduce penalty tempering, which modulates constraint barriers while preserving the objective landscape over feasible solutions. Empirically, our method consistently outperforms SCIP across all four benchmarks, matches or exceeds Gurobi on two of four tasks within a 200-second budget, and is substantially more robust to distribution shift than learning-based methods. Furthermore, on MIPLIB 2017 instances, our framework remains competitive with classical solvers without any problem-specific tuning.

</details>


### [134] [TRACER: Persistent Regularization for Robust Multimodal Finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29380)
*Hesam Asadollahzadeh,Feng Liu,Christopher Leckie,Sarah M. Erfani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对多模态对比微调中灾难性遗忘导致OOD鲁棒性下降的问题，提出了理论框架和解决方案TRACER，通过加权移动平均教师（WMA）替代标准EMA教师，结合对比学习与多视角蒸馏，在CLIP微调中显著提升了OOD准确率和校准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流多模态模型微调策略常导致OOD鲁棒性下降（灾难性遗忘），现有正则化方法中自蒸馏效果较好，但广泛使用的EMA教师存在坍塌问题，亟待解决。

Method: 建立多模态对比微调的理论框架，推导出封闭解和几何分解；提出TRACER方法，包含：(1) WMA教师替代EMA教师，在有限视野内保持持久正则化力，实现任务子空间无偏收敛并保留正交知识；(2) 对比学习与WMA引导的多视角蒸馏相结合。

Result: 在CLIP微调上进行大量实验，三种骨干架构上均取得一致的OOD准确率和校准性能提升，全面的消融实验验证了TRACER的理论原则性和对超参数的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: TRACER是一种有原则且鲁棒的对比编码器正则化方法，有效缓解了多模态微调中的灾难性遗忘问题，代码已开源。

Abstract: Mainstream strategies for finetuning pretrained multimodal models often degrade out-of-distribution (OOD) robustness, a phenomenon known as catastrophic forgetting. In this paper, we develop a theoretical framework for multimodal contrastive finetuning, yielding closed-form solutions and a geometric decomposition for each strategy. This framework shows that self-distillation is more effective than other regularization approaches to retain the knowledge of the pretrained model. Our analysis reveals a largely overlooked limitation: standard Exponential Moving Average (EMA) teachers, widely used in robust finetuning, suffer from collapse. To solve this, we prove that a Weighted Moving Average (WMA) teacher maintains a persistent regularizing force over finite horizons and yields bias-free convergence in the task subspace while preserving orthogonal knowledge. These insights motivate **TRACER** (**T**rajectory-**R**obust **A**nchoring for **C**ontrastive **E**ncoder **R**egularization), which combines contrastive learning with WMA-guided multi-perspective distillation. Extensive experiments on CLIP finetuning demonstrate consistent OOD accuracy and calibration gains across three backbone architectures, and comprehensive ablations confirm that TRACER is both principled and robust to hyperparameter choices. Code is available at [https://github.com/HesamAsad/TRACER](https://github.com/HesamAsad/TRACER).

</details>


### [135] [Information-Directed Offline-to-Online Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29405)
*Keru Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出信息导向采样（IDS）框架，通过条件互信息量化离线数据后的残差不确定性，并证明IDS在离线到在线决策中能继承Thompson采样的信息比界，在贝叶斯线性奖励模型下取得改进的遗憾界，实验验证了IDS在离线数据有信息但存在偏置或低概率残差不确定性时的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线数据集能减少不确定性，但并不能消除探索需求，只是改变了需要探索的内容。现有方法通常从离线数据热启动策略，但缺乏对残差不确定性的系统量化和最优探索的理论指导。

Method: 通过条件互信息形式化残差不确定性，提出参数化族信息导向采样（IDS），通过比率证书证明IDS继承Thompson采样的信息比界，在贝叶斯线性奖励模型中推导出显式的遗憾界，并通过理论和实验分析离线数据信息丰富但存在偏置/低概率残差不确定性的温启动场景。

Result: IDS在贝叶斯线性奖励模型中达到$\widetilde O\!\left(Hd\min\left\{\sqrt T,\,T\sqrt{C^\dagger_{β,\mathrm{IDS}_0}(N,T)/N}\right\}\right)$遗憾界；在温启动场景中IDS选择信息探针而Thompson采样从不选择，实现常数因子的贝叶斯遗憾分离；D4RL实验验证IDS在离线数据信息丰富但存在偏置/低概率残差不确定性时最有效。

Conclusion: 条件互信息形式化离线到在线决策中的残差不确定性是有效的，IDS框架能系统性地指导探索，在离线强化学习、离线黑盒优化和贝叶斯优化等存在残差不确定性的场景中具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: Decision-making from offline datasets typically warm-starts a policy or score model from fixed offline data and then refines it with limited online interaction. Offline data reduces uncertainty, but it does not remove the need for exploration; it changes what remains to be explored. We formalise this residual uncertainty by the conditional mutual information $I(χ;τ_{1:T}\mid\mathcal{D}_N)$ between a learning target $χ$ and the online trajectories after conditioning on the offline dataset. This view leads naturally to information-directed sampling (IDS), a family parameterised by $η\ge 0$ that selects actions by trading off instantaneous regret against information gain. We prove a generic offline-to-online Bayesian regret bound for IDS through a ratio certificate: any information-ratio bound satisfied by a reference Thompson-sampling policy over the same randomised policy class is inherited by IDS. In a known-dynamics Bayesian linear-reward model, the conditional mutual information has a log-determinant form, and vanilla IDS ($η=0$) satisfies $\widetilde O\!\left(Hd\min\left\{\sqrt T,\,T\sqrt{C^\dagger_{β,\mathrm{IDS}_0}(N,T)/N}\right\}\right),$ where the coverage coefficient is tied to the visitation distribution induced by vanilla IDS itself. We also identify a warm-start regime with a dominated but informative probe in which vanilla IDS selects the probe while Thompson sampling never does, giving a constant-factor Bayesian regret separation. Controlled bandit experiments and D4RL offline-to-online RL experiments validate this mechanism: IDS is most beneficial when offline data is informative but leaves biased or low-probability residual uncertainty that targeted online actions can resolve, a regime shared by offline RL, offline black-box optimization, and Bayesian optimization.

</details>


### [136] [The Good, the Bad, and the Ugly of Markov Boundary for Tabular Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29411)
*Shu Wan,Abhinav Gorantla,Huan Liu,K. Selçuk Candan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究马尔可夫边界在表格预测中的实际效用。理论上，马尔可夫边界是最优预测的最小特征集，但在SCM3K基准（3450个任务）上，使用六种回归器评估后发现：使用真实边界能显著提升预测性能（尤其在特征空间大且稀疏时），但先因果发现再特征选择的实际流程并未奏效——现有估计器在到达边界最有效的区域前就已耗尽计算预算，且即使运行完成也很少能超越全特征集。原因有三：发现算法优化结构恢复而非预测、假阳/假阴性预测代价不对称、精确边界并非唯一能超越全特征的子集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理论上马尔可夫边界是预测目标变量所需的最小充分特征集，但实践中现代回归器仍使用全特征集进行训练。作者希望检验马尔可夫边界在表格预测任务中的真实效用，探究理论承诺与实际表现之间的差距。

Method: 在SCM3K基准（包含3450个任务、特征数40-1000、6种SCM族）上，使用六种不同回归器，对比三种情况：全特征集训练、仅用真实马尔可夫边界特征训练、先通过因果发现恢复边界再训练的完整流程。分析预测性能差异并诊断原因。

Result: 使用真实马尔可夫边界能显著提升预测性能，且特征空间越大越稀疏提升越明显。但因果发现+边界选择的完整流程未能超越全特征集：发现算法在到达边界最有效区域前已耗尽计算预算，且即使运行完成也鲜有超越。三个根本原因：发现算法优化结构恢复而非预测、假阳与假阴性预测代价不对称、精确边界只是众多能超越全特征集的子集之一。

Conclusion: 马尔可夫边界在预测上有理论价值但实际应用存在鸿沟。未来应开发面向预测对齐的特征选择方法，以及能学习利用因果结构的表格模型，而非简单套用因果发现后接特征选择的流水线。

Abstract: Under standard graphical assumptions, the Markov boundary of a target variable is the smallest set of features that renders every other feature redundant. Once the boundary is observed, the target is conditionally independent of the rest of the table. This is a tempting object for tabular prediction, since it names exactly the columns a model should need. Yet modern regressors are still trained on the full feature set. We ask whether the Markov boundary is genuinely useful for prediction on SCM3K, a 3,450-task synthetic SCM benchmark with feature counts from 40 to 1000 and six SCM families, evaluated with six regressors. The answer is more nuanced than the theory suggests. Restricting a regressor to the oracle boundary often improves prediction substantially, and the improvement grows as the feature space becomes larger and sparser. But the natural pipeline of recovering the boundary with causal discovery and training on the recovered mask does not deliver. Existing estimators exhaust the compute budget before reaching the regime where the boundary helps most, and even where they run they rarely beat the full feature set. We trace this to three causes. Discovery optimizes structural recovery rather than prediction. False negatives and false positives carry sharply asymmetric predictive cost. The exact boundary is only one of many feature sets that beat all features. We then develop what these facts imply for prediction-aligned feature selection and for tabular models that learn to use causal structure.

</details>


### [137] [Forget Less, Generalize More: Unifying Temporal and Structural Adaptation for Dynamic Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29453)
*Qian Chang,Ciprian Doru Giurcaneanu,Runsong Jia,Xia Li,Guoping Hu,Xiufeng Cheng,Jinqing Yang,Mengjia Wu,Yi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出双尺度保持动态（DSRD）框架，通过保持性表示状态联合建模时间动态和结构传播，在14个真实基准上实现链接预测和节点分类任务的最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态图表示学习方法采用固定的时间衰减方案或预定的结构传播深度，限制了其在不同交互频率和拓扑特征图上的泛化能力。

Method: DSRD包含两个关键组件：(i) 具有双尺度适应的保持状态，在单一循环公式中联合建模时间动态和结构传播；(ii) 自适应衰减核，使用可学习的时间敏感参数自动平衡短期响应和长期记忆。理论分析建立了事件级并行聚合与高效循环状态更新之间的等价性，并提供了所学动态的稳定性和有界性保证。

Result: 在14个真实基准上的大量实验表明，DSRD在链接预测和节点分类任务中持续达到最先进性能，并在直推和归纳设置下均表现出强泛化能力。

Conclusion: DSRD框架通过双尺度保持动态机制有效联合建模时间与结构信息，在动态图表示学习中实现了更强的泛化性能和理论保证。

Abstract: Representation learning on dynamic graphs requires capturing complex dependencies that evolve across both time and structure. Existing approaches typically adopt fixed temporal decay schemes or predetermined structural propagation depths, limiting their ability to generalize across graphs with diverse interaction frequencies and topological characteristics. We propose Dual-Scale Retentive Dynamics (DSRD), a unified framework that maintains a retentive representation state encoding both temporal memory and structural context. DSRD introduces two key components: (i) a retentive state with dual-scale adaptation that jointly models temporal dynamics and structural propagation within a single recurrent formulation, and (ii) adaptive decay kernels with learnable time-sensitivity parameters that automatically balance short-term responsiveness and long-term retention based on the underlying interaction patterns. We provide theoretical analysis establishing the equivalence between event-wise parallel aggregation and efficient recurrent state updates, as well as stability and boundedness guarantees for the learned dynamics. Extensive experiments on 14 real-world benchmarks demonstrate that DSRD consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance on both link prediction and node classification tasks, with strong generalization across transductive and inductive settings.

</details>


### [138] [A Full-Pipeline Framework for Evaluating Membership Inference Attacks in Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29454)
*Ding Chen,Xinwen Cheng,Xuyang Zhong,Xinping Chen,Xiaolin Huang,Chen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个系统化评估框架，用于表征不同上下文（数据、架构、算法、后训练模块）对成员推理攻击（MIA）效能的影响，并通过标准化威胁模型和多种评估指标，揭示了MIA方法对威胁模型和评估指标的高度敏感性，最终提供实用指南和审计工具包。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MIA研究缺乏系统性框架来评估不同上下文的效能影响，导致在基准上表现良好的算法在面对真实数据集的具体特征时可能统计上失效，从业者无法获得可操作的隐私风险评估指导。

Method: 提出了一个涵盖数据、架构、算法和后训练模块的完整机器学习管道的评估框架；采用三种互补指标（平衡准确率、低FPR下的TPR、低FNR下的TNR）以应对不同误分类成本场景；形式化了两种标准化威胁模型，并将现有MIA适配为相应变体以确保公平比较。

Result: 大量实证评估表明，特定MIA方法的效能对假设的威胁模型和所选评估指标高度敏感。

Conclusion: 将研究发现提炼为可操作指南，并提供了一个即用型审计工具包，赋能从业者进行更优的隐私评估。

Abstract: While Membership Inference Attacks (MIAs) are the prevailing method for identifying training data, their application has expanded into privacy auditing and machine unlearning. Nevertheless, the field lacks a systematic framework for evaluating how different contexts affect MIA efficacy. Without such a characterization, practitioners risk deploying algorithms that perform well on benchmarks but become statistically irrelevant when faced with the nuances of specific, real-world datasets. To bridge this gap and provide actionable insights, we introduce a comprehensive evaluation framework that systematically characterizes privacy risks across the entire machine learning pipeline, spanning data, architectures, algorithms, and post-training modules. Designed to inherently capture diverse operational contexts, our framework rigorously evaluates state-of-the-art MIAs across a broad spectrum of training configurations. To account for varying misclassification costs in real-world deployments, we employ three complementary metrics: Balanced Accuracy for symmetric costs, alongside TPR at low FPR (or TNR at low FNR) for asymmetric scenarios where false alarms or missed detections are strictly penalized. Furthermore, recognizing that existing MIAs assume divergent adversary capabilities, we formalize two standardized threat models and adapt these attacks into corresponding variants to ensure an equitable benchmark. Extensive empirical evaluations demonstrate that the efficacy of specific MIA methodologies is highly sensitive to the assumed threat models and chosen evaluation metrics. Ultimately, we distill these findings into actionable guidelines and provide a ready-to-use auditing toolkit, empowering practitioners to conduct better privacy assessments.

</details>


### [139] [Honest Lying: Understanding Memory Confabulation in Reflexive Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29463)
*Prakhar Dixit,Sadia Kamal,Tim Oates*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Reflexion-style智能体的一个系统性缺陷：它们依赖自身生成的反思作为记忆，但会存储自信且错误的解释，导致在多轮任务中持续重复错误。作者提出**记忆虚构（memory confabulation）**概念，并引入**反思重复率（RRR）**指标检测这一失败模式。通过将开放式自我诊断替换为程序化轨迹级故障信号提取，在ALFWorld中正确目标提及率从0%提升至86%，RRR从0.64降至0.10，并成功解决了16个冻结环境中的3个。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Reflexion-style智能体假设其能准确诊断自身失败，但作者发现这一假设可能系统性失效：智能体会存储自信但错误的任务解释，并在环境重置后依然基于这些错误记忆行动，形成**记忆虚构**（memory confabulation）问题。

Method: 1) 提出**反思重复率（RRR）**指标，通过日志检测智能体反复依赖错误反思内容的行为；2) 在ALFWorld和HumanEval上识别出16个冻结环境（ALFWorld）和4个类似案例（HumanEval），其中0/121条反思提及正确目标对象；3) 提出**程序化轨迹级故障信号提取**（替代开放式自我诊断），将正确的失败信号注入反思过程。

Result: 在ALFWorld的16个冻结环境中：正确目标对象提及率从0%提升至86%，RRR从0.64降至0.10，成功解决了其中3个环境。HumanEval上发现4个类似案例。

Conclusion: 反思式记忆可能强化而非纠正错误信念，单纯的自我生成反思不可靠；程序化提取轨迹级失败信号是更有效的替代方案。

Abstract: Reflexion-style agents rely on self-generated reflections as memory, implicitly assuming that agents can accurately diagnose their own failures.We show that this assumption can fail systematically: across ALFWorld and HumanEval, agents store confident but incorrect interpretations of the task and continue acting on them across trials,even though the environment resets to the correct task each time. We call this failure mode memory confabulation and introduce the Reflection Repetition Rate (RRR), a log-based metric that detects repeated reliance on incorrect reflective content.Using RRR, we identify 16 frozen environments in ALFWorld, where 0 of 121 reflections mention the correct target object, and 4 analogous cases in HumanEval. Our mitigation replaces open-ended self-diagnosis with programmatic extraction of trajectory-level failure signals, increasing correct object mention from 0% to 86%, reducing RRR from 0.64 to 0.10, and solving 3 of 16 frozen ALFWorld environments, suggesting that reflective memory can reinforce false beliefs rather than correct them.

</details>


### [140] [Composing Non-Conjugate Factor Graphs with Closed-Form Variational Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29467)
*Mykola Lukashchuk,Kyrylo Yemets,Wouter M. Kouw,Dmitry Bagaev,İsmail Şenöz,Jeff Beck,Bert de Vries*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了由五种因子图原语（双线性因子、指数链接、Gamma先验、高斯似然、等式节点）组成的任何概率模型都能保持闭式变分消息传递，并展示了通过堆叠路由层可编码任意决策树，实现通用函数逼近与闭式推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将概率构建块堆叠成更深层架构通常会破坏闭式推断，本文旨在探索如何保留闭式推断能力。

Method: 识别五种因子图原语，证明在其组成的模型中，在平均场分解下消息族保持不变（高斯变量消息仍为高斯，精度变量消息仍为Gamma），非共轭接口（指数链接）通过高斯矩母函数和Gamma充分统计量保持可处理。通过静态集成、输入依赖门控、分裂分支路由展示组合深度，并证明路由层堆叠可编码任意决策树。

Result: 在集成时间序列预测中，该框架产生贝叶斯混合专家模型，门控函数通过推断而非学习获得，在五个基准数据集上提供了校准的不确定性估计。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架成功保留了深层概率模型中的闭式推断能力，并具有通用函数逼近的理论保证和实际应用价值。

Abstract: Stacking probabilistic building blocks into deeper architectures typically breaks closed-form inference. We show that closed-form inference can be preserved. We identify five factor-graph primitives: a bilinear factor, an exponential link, a Gamma prior, a Gaussian likelihood, and an equality node, and prove that any model composed from them admits closed-form variational message passing. The construction works because each primitive preserves a small set of message families: under mean-field factorization, messages on Gaussian variables remain Gaussian and messages on precision variables remain Gamma, while the only non-conjugate interface, the exponential link, remains tractable through the Gaussian moment-generating function and the sufficient statistics of the Gamma family. We demonstrate composition at increasing depth, from static ensembles through input-dependent gating to split-branch routing, and show that stacking routing layers encodes arbitrary decision trees, establishing universal function approximation with closed-form inference. Applied to ensemble time-series forecasting, the framework yields a Bayesian mixture of experts in which gating functions are inferred rather than learned, providing calibrated uncertainty over expert selection across five benchmark datasets.

</details>


### [141] [Access Sets Matter: Budgeting Expert Reads for Scalable Weight-Space Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29489)
*Yuanyi Wang,Yanggan Gu,Su Lu,Yifan Yang,Zhaoyi Yan,Congkai Xie,Jianmin Wu,Hongxia Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MergePipe是一个预算感知的执行层，将LLM合并问题转化为专家访问集问题，通过选择性读取参数块在保持合并质量的同时大幅降低I/O开销，实现高达11倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统权重空间模型合并方法将合并视为检查点上的代数操作，但在LLM规模下，读取所有专家权重成为主要瓶颈，需要一种预算感知的合并策略来减少I/O开销。

Method: MergePipe将LLM合并建模为专家访问集问题：给定合并算子和共享权重坐标系下的检查点家族，在显式I/O预算下选择要访问的专家delta块。系统通过索引参数块、构建确定性访问计划并执行预算合并，生成可重放的执行清单。预算计划在构造上保证预算合理性，在满预算时恢复完整读取合并。

Result: 在Qwen和Llama合并任务上，MergePipe将专家读取I/O降低一个数量级，实现最高11倍加速。预算扫描显示参数偏差仅为O(10^{-3})量级，且下游基准测试无单调退化。

Conclusion: MergePipe通过预算感知的专家访问策略，在保持合并质量的同时显著降低I/O开销，证明了选择性访问策略在大规模模型合并中的有效性。

Abstract: Weight-space model merging is usually formulated as an algebraic operation on checkpoints, yet at LLM scale the limiting resource is often the set of expert weights that must be read. We introduce MergePipe, a budget-aware execution layer that casts LLM merging as an \emph{expert access-set} problem: given a merge operator and a checkpoint family in a shared weight coordinate system, choose which expert delta blocks to access under an explicit I/O budget. MergePipe indexes parameter blocks, builds deterministic access plans, and executes the induced budgeted merge with replayable manifests. The plan is budget-sound by construction and recovers the full-read merge at full budget; for fixed-coefficient additive operators, the omitted-update error is bounded by the norm of omitted deltas. Across Qwen and Llama merging workloads, MergePipe reduces expert-read I/O by up to an order of magnitude and achieves up to $11\times$ speedups. Representative budget sweeps show $O(10^{-3})$ parameter deviation from full-read merges and no monotonic degradation on downstream benchmarks.

</details>


### [142] [Gradient Perturbation: Learning to Perturb Gradients for Adaptive Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29494)
*Hua Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了梯度扰动的统一框架，揭示了SAM、梯度裁剪、梯度噪声注入等方法均可视为特定形式的梯度扰动。基于此，提出LPG方法，自适应地在类别级别扰动logit梯度，实现类别感知训练，在平衡分类、长尾分类和噪声标签学习任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前向传播链上的扰动（特征扰动、logit扰动、标签扰动）已被广泛研究，但反向传播链上的梯度扰动缺乏系统性研究。本文旨在建立梯度扰动的统一框架，并探索其应用潜力。

Method: 提出Learning to Perturb Gradients (LPG)，自适应地在类别级别扰动logit梯度。通过放大某类梯度范数来增强学习（正增强），抑制某类梯度范数来防止过拟合（负增强）。利用PAC-Bayesian分析建立了梯度扰动界与泛化保证之间的理论联系。

Result: 在平衡分类、长尾分类和噪声标签学习任务中，LPG一致优于现有方法，且可作为插件模块与现有方法结合使用。

Conclusion: 梯度扰动是一个重要但被忽视的研究方向，LPG通过类别感知的自适应梯度扰动有效提升了模型泛化能力，且具有良好的兼容性和迁移性。

Abstract: Deep neural network training involves both forward propagation (from features through logits to loss) and backward propagation (from loss through gradients to parameter updates). While perturbations along the forward chain, including feature perturbation, logit perturbation, and label perturbation, have been extensively studied, the backward chain's gradient perturbation has received little systematic investigation. In this paper, we establish a unified framework for gradient perturbation, revealing that existing methods such as Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM), gradient clipping, and gradient noise injection can all be interpreted as imposing specific forms of gradient perturbation. Analogous to the recently proposed Logit Perturbation Learning (LPL), we conjecture that amplifying the gradient norm for a class acts as positive augmentation (enhancing learning), while dampening it acts as negative augmentation (suppressing overfitting). Based on these observations, we propose Learning to Perturb Gradients (LPG), which adaptively perturbs logit-level gradients at the class level to achieve category-aware training. We also establish theoretical connections between gradient perturbation bounds and generalization guarantees via PAC-Bayesian analysis. Experiments on balanced classification, long-tail classification, and noisy label learning demonstrate that LPG consistently outperforms existing methods and can be combined with them as a plug-in module.

</details>


### [143] [On-Policy Replay for Continual Supervised Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29495)
*Yan Chen,Taojie Zhu,Meng Zhang,Xin Chen,Jiaqi Huang,Dongyang Xu,Yizhi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出On-Policy Replay（OPR）方法，通过在少量历史提示上使用最新模型生成回复、经任务奖励筛选后作为普通SFT样本回放，在不引入教师模型、辅助损失或在线蒸馏的情况下，显著缓解持续SFT中的灾难性遗忘。在多个7-8B指令微调模型上，OPR将后向迁移（BWT）从-13.93提升至-0.65（10%回放预算）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续监督微调（SFT）在适应下游任务流时存在严重的灾难性遗忘问题。现有基于on-policy信号的方法（如自蒸馏）虽能缓解遗忘，但引入了额外的前向传播、调度敏感性和教师风格漂移。作者希望在不增加这些复杂性的前提下，利用on-policy信号来缓解遗忘。

Method: OPR方法：在持续学习过程中，对最新模型检查点在少量历史提示上进行推理（rollout），通过任务奖励筛选生成结果，然后将筛选后的（提示，模型回复）对作为普通SFT样本进行回放训练。不涉及教师模型、辅助损失或在线蒸馏。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct、Qwen3-8B、Llama3.1-8B-Instruct三个模型上，使用TRACE基准测试，OPR持续降低遗忘。在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct的连续SFT中，BWT从-13.93提升至-0.65（10%回放预算）和-2.29（1%预算），相较Vanilla Replay基线|BWT|降低46%。三个模型上均有42-46%的降低。

Conclusion: OPR的核心有效成分是on-policy分布，而非回复质量本身（低分回放性能低于Vanilla Replay证明了这一点）。OPR与先前的on-policy蒸馏方法可通过KL收缩解释统一在一个轴上。

Abstract: Continual supervised fine-tuning (SFT) is the de facto recipe for adapting large language models (LLMs) to a stream of downstream tasks, but it suffers from catastrophic forgetting of earlier capabilities. Recent work shows that on-policy signals -- training on the model's own outputs -- reduce forgetting more reliably than off-policy supervision. Existing on-policy methods route this signal through a new training objective (e.g., self-distillation losses with a teacher copy), inheriting an extra forward pass, schedule sensitivity, and stylistic drift from the teacher.We instead route the on-policy signal through the training data source. Our method, On-Policy Replay (OPR), rolls out the most recent checkpoint on a small budget of historical prompts, filters the generations by a task reward, and replays the surviving (prompt, model response) pairs as ordinary SFT examples. There is no teacher, no auxiliary loss, and no on-the-fly distillation. Across three 7--8B instruction-tuned backbones (Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, Qwen3-8B, Llama3.1-8B-Instruct) on the TRACE continual-learning benchmark, OPR consistently reduces forgetting; on the sharpest stress test (Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, Sequential SFT BWT -13.93), OPR lifts BWT to -0.65 at a 10% replay budget and to -2.29 at a 1% budget -- a 46% reduction in |BWT| over a tuned Vanilla Replay baseline, with 42--46% reductions observed across all three backbones. We give a KL-shrinkage interpretation that places OPR and prior on-policy distillation methods on a single axis, and we present a counterintuitive finding that explains why Vanilla Replay is already a strong baseline: low-score replay is uniformly worse than Vanilla Replay, demonstrating that the active ingredient in OPR is the on-policy distribution, not the response quality alone.Our code is available at https://github.com/Yancey2024/OnPolicyReplay.

</details>


### [144] [Convex Basins in Single-Index Model Loss Landscapes: Applications to Robust Recovery under Strong Adversarial Corruption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29497)
*Santanu Das,Sagnik Chatterjee,Jatin Batra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次针对带有非单调链接函数（如GeLU和Swish）的高斯单指标模型(SIMs)，在存在重尾噪声和对抗性污染的情况下，提出了具有近线性样本和时间复杂度的鲁棒恢复算法，建立了收敛误差为O(σ√ε)的保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鲁棒恢复技术局限于线性回归、严格单调链接函数或相位恢复等问题，无法处理现代门控神经架构中自然出现的非对称非单调链接函数（如GeLU和Swish），因此亟需填补这一理论空白。

Method: 核心贡献在于对抗污染下高斯平方损失景观的新结构理解：证明了一类非线性非单调SIMs在真实参数周围存在维度无关的常半径凸盆地，并通过鲁棒谱初始化可高效到达；在此基础上，利用该结构提供原则性热启动，驱动鲁棒梯度下降算法收敛。

Result: 算法在Õ(d)样本和Õ(nd)时间内实现最终估计误差O(σ√ε)，其中ε为污染比例，且同时适用于非单调链接函数和对抗性污染场景。

Conclusion: 该工作首次为一大类非线性非单调SIMs建立了鲁棒恢复保证，弥补了先前方法无法同时处理对抗污染和通用非单调链接函数的缺陷。

Abstract: We study the problem of robustly learning Gaussian Single Index Models (SIMs) in the presence of heavy-tailed noise and a constant fraction of adversarially corrupted covariates and responses. Prior work on robust recovery has considered settings such as linear regression (Pensia et al., JASA 2024), strictly monotonic link functions (Awasthi et al., NeurIPS 2022), and phase retrieval (Buna and Rebeschini, AISTATS 2025). However, these techniques do not extend to generic asymmetric non-monotonic link functions such as \textsc{GeLU} and \textsc{Swish}, which arise naturally as scalar primitives in modern gated neural architectures. We close this gap by giving the first robust recovery algorithm with near-linear sample and time complexity for generic non-monotonic link functions, thereby establishing the first robust recovery guarantees for a broad family of nonlinear SIMs for which \textit{no guarantees were previously known}. Our central contribution is a new structural understanding of the Gaussian squared-loss landscape under adversarial contamination. Crucially, we prove that for a broad class of nonlinear non-monotonic SIMs, a dimension-independent, constant-radius convex basin exists around the ground truth and is efficiently reachable via robust spectral initialization even under adversarial contamination. Prior works fail to establish both guarantees simultaneously, thereby either breaking down under adversarial contamination or failing to handle generic non-monotonic link functions. Together, these structural insights yield a principled warm start for robust gradient descent that provably converges to a final estimation error of $O(σ\sqrtε)$ in $\tilde{O}(nd)$ time with $\tilde{O}(d)$ samples, where $ε$ is the contamination fraction.

</details>


### [145] [Quotient DAGs for Off-Policy Evaluation:Forward-Flow Importance Sampling and Exact Slate Propensities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29500)
*Ziwen Xie,Shaowen Xiang,Hongyu He,Dianbo Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种商DAG（quotient-DAG）视角，通过合并对评估等价的轨迹历史，使用目标-行为前向流比率在合并图上分配权重。针对推荐系统中自回归生成长序列但评估仅依赖无序集合的问题，提出了Forward-DP子集DAG动态规划算法，能够在不进行阶乘枚举的情况下精确计算无序倾向性分数，从而提升离线策略评估的效率和方差性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准重要性采样方法在评估自回归生成策略时，会将生成过程的具体顺序细节视为有意义的信息，即使评估目标（如奖励函数）实际上只依赖于无序的结果（如无序的商品集合），这引入了多余的噪声方差，且精确计算无序倾向性需要枚举所有生成顺序，存在计算上的不可行性。

Method: 引入商DAG（quotient-DAG）视角，将评估等价的历史轨迹合并为等价类，在合并图上利用目标策略与行为策略之间的前向流比率分配权重。针对推荐中的集合充分下一项接口（set-sufficient next-item interface），进一步提出Forward-DP，一种基于子集DAG的动态规划算法，在不枚举所有排列的情况下精确计算无序倾向性分数。

Result: 所提出的Forward-DP能够高效计算精确的无序倾向性分数，避免阶乘级枚举开销，为基于倾向性的上下文相关自回归推荐日志器的离线评估和模型选择提供了实用的基础工具。

Conclusion: 商DAG视角为处理评估目标与生成过程之间的结构不匹配问题提供了通用框架，Forward-DP则在实际的推荐场景中验证了该方法的可行性和有效性，弥合了自回归生成模型与无序评估之间的计算差距。

Abstract: Off-policy evaluation estimates how a target policy would perform using data collected by a different behavior policy, which is crucial when online testing is costly or risky, such as in recommendation or healthcare. Standard importance sampling reweights each logged trajectory, but it can treat details of the generation process as meaningful even when the evaluation target ignores them: for example, an autoregressive slate recommender may generate an ordered sequence of items while the reward and downstream estimator depend only on the unordered slate. This creates nuisance variance and a computational gap, since exact unordered slate propensities require summing over all generation orders. We introduce a quotient-DAG view that merges histories equivalent for evaluation and assigns weights using target-to-behavior forward-flow ratios on the merged graph. For slate recommendation under a set-sufficient next-item interface, this yields Forward-DP, a subset-DAG dynamic program that computes exact unordered propensities without factorial enumeration. The resulting propensity primitive enables practical propensity-based evaluation and model selection for context-dependent autoregressive slate loggers.

</details>


### [146] [K-FinHallu: A Hallucination Detection Benchmark for Multi-Turn RAG in Korean Finance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29523)
*Eunbyeol Cho,Yunseung Lee,Mirae Kim,Jeewon Yang,Youngjun Kwak,Edward Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: K-FinHallu是首个韩语多轮金融RAG幻觉检测基准，通过层级分类法注入幻觉并评测多种LLM，发现即使最强模型在细粒度金融诊断和合理拒绝上仍有困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检测基准局限于单轮、英语任务，缺乏针对韩语金融领域多轮对话和语言-监管细微差别的考量，而幻觉问题阻碍了LLM在高风险金融场景中的实际部署。

Method: 从真实韩语金融文档构建多轮对话，基于上下文可回答性提出层级幻觉分类法，并注入幻觉；评测前沿与开源LLM作为幻觉检测器的表现，并对8B模型进行微调。

Result: 最强模型在细粒度金融诊断和拒绝行为上表现不佳；微调后的8B模型性能接近前沿LLM，但合理拒绝（justified abstention）是所有模型中最薄弱的维度。

Conclusion: 现有LLM在韩语金融多轮RAG场景下幻觉检测能力不足，尤其是合理拒绝方面亟待改进，K-FinHallu为后续研究提供了重要评估基准。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have advanced financial automation through Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG), yet hallucinations remain a critical barrier to deployment in high-stakes environments. Existing benchmarks focus on single-turn, English-centric tasks, leaving the multi-turn dynamics and linguistic-regulatory nuances of the Korean financial domain unaddressed. We introduce K-FinHallu, the first benchmark for hallucination detection in multi-turn Korean financial RAG. We construct multi-turn dialogues from authentic Korean financial documents and inject hallucinations under a proposed hierarchical taxonomy based on context answerability that explicitly accounts for justified abstention. Benchmarking frontier and open-source LLMs as hallucination detectors, we find that even the strongest models struggle with fine-grained financial diagnostics and refusal behavior. While fine-tuning an 8B model on our training split yields performance competitive with frontier LLMs, justified abstention remains the weakest axis across all evaluated models.

</details>


### [147] [AsymVLM: Asymmetric Token Pruning for Efficient Vision-Language Model Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29535)
*Yilin Feng,Ahmed Burak Gulhan,Mahmut Taylan Kandemir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AsymVLM提出针对视觉和文本token的**非对称压缩**方法：对视觉token在预填充阶段进行高比率剪枝（自适应预算+学习重要性评分器），对文本token仅在解码阶段超出预算时进行时间阈值驱逐。该方法在节省最高54% FLOPs的同时，在文档/图表理解任务上超越现有方法2-3%，并在文本主导场景下优于标准LLM缓存压缩方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM压缩方法对视觉和文本token采用统一处理，忽略了两种模态的根本性差异：视觉token空间冗余高且预填充阶段占主导，文本token具有因果依赖性且解码阶段不断累积。

Method: 1) 视觉token预填充前剪枝：使用学习的重要性评分器，配合每个样本的自适应预算，进行激进剪枝；2) 文本token驱逐：解码阶段采用基于时间阈值的驱逐策略，仅在token超出固定预算时执行。

Result: AsymVLM取得最高54%的FLOPs节省；在文档和图表理解任务上超越现有方法2-3%；在整体基准上保持竞争性准确率；文本主导场景下显著优于标准LLM缓存压缩方法。

Conclusion: 针对VLM中视觉与文本token不对称特性的非对称压缩是更有效的方案，能够在高计算效率节省下保持甚至提升下游任务性能，尤其在空间局部化且查询特定的视觉任务中优势明显。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) process thousands of visual tokens per image alongside comparatively few text tokens, yet existing compression methods treat both modalities uniformly. We observe that the two modalities have fundamentally different properties: vision tokens are spatially redundant and dominate prefill, while text tokens are causally dependent and accumulate during decoding. Based on this asymmetry, we propose and empirically evaluate AsymVLM, which applies aggressive pruning to vision tokens before prefill using a learned importance scorer with per-sample adaptive budgeting, and temporal threshold-based eviction to text tokens only when they exceed a fixed budget. Our experiments indicate that AsymVLM achieves the highest FLOPs savings (up to 54%) among state-of-the-art methods while outperforming existing approaches by 2--3% on document and chart understanding tasks where visual information is spatially localized and query-specific, and maintaining competitive accuracy on holistic benchmarks. In text-dominated scenarios, our eviction strategy substantially outperforms standard LLM cache compression methods by adapting to the short-context nature of VLM.

</details>


### [148] [SCOPE: A Lightweight-training LLM Framework for Air Traffic Control Readback Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29543)
*Qihan Deng,Minghua Zhang,Yang Yang,Zhenyu Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SCOPE框架，一种轻量级训练的LLM方法，通过耦合开放集分类器和上下文学习机制，在冻结的LLM上实现高效准确的空管复诵监控，在少样本条件下达到91.05%的开放集检测准确率和96.63%的异常复诵纠正率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空管复诵异常是约80%航空事故的诱因，且随着交通量增长和认知负荷增加而加剧。传统方法难以泛化，现有LLM方案存在部署和计算瓶颈，亟需高效实用的自动化复诵监控方案。

Method: 提出SCOPE框架，核心是将开放集插件分类器与精心设计的上下文学习机制相结合，基于冻结的LLM运行，仅需轻量级训练即可实现语义推理。

Result: 在半合成通信数据集上，少样本设置下开放集检测准确率91.05%，异常复诵纠正率96.63%，优于最强基线，同时保持低延迟满足运行环境需求。

Conclusion: SCOPE框架为可解释、可控制的空管复诵监控提供了一条实用路径，兼具高精度和低延迟优势。

Abstract: Pilot readback of Air Traffic Control (ATC) voice instructions is a primary safeguard against miscommunication in air transportation. However, readback anomalies remain implicated in approximately 80% of aviation incidents. This vulnerability is further exacerbated by rising traffic volume and elevated cognitive workload, thereby motivating automated readback monitoring by machine. Traditional rule-based and machine learning approaches struggle to generalize across the highly variable and evolving phraseology of air traffic controller-pilot communications. While Large Language Models (LLMs) have opened a new avenue through their strong reasoning and generalization capabilities, existing approaches still face deployment and computational barriers in practice. In this work, we propose Semantic reasoning for Communication via Open-set Plug-in with Examples (SCOPE), a novel lightweight-training LLM framework that advances both the efficiency and accuracy of machine-based ATC readback monitoring. The core idea is to couple a plug-in open-set classifier with a carefully designed in-context learning mechanism on top of a frozen LLM. Extensive experiments on the semi-synthetic communication dataset show that SCOPE attains superior accuracy while delivering the low-latency response required for operational environments. Under a few-shot setting, SCOPE achieves 91.05% accuracy in open-set detection and corrects 96.63% of anomalous readbacks, thereby outperforming the strongest available baselines while providing explanations for its decisions. These findings demonstrate the potential of our framework as a practical pathway toward interpretable and controllable ATC readback monitoring.

</details>


### [149] [Why Larger Models Learn More: Effects of Capacity, Interference, and Rare-Task Retention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29548)
